We evaluate a battery of methods, including constant predictors, gradient-boosted trees, retrieval methods, fine-tuned encoder language models (LMs), prompted decoder LMs, and knowledge-augmented agents. Our results show that more sophisticated LM and agentic approaches do not consistently outperform the strongest feature-based baseline. All model families struggle to identify zero awards and to calibrate high-award predictions, with further degradation on the Challenging test view, making ECtHR-NPD a challenging testbed for current state-of-the-art open-weight and proprietary LMs.

</details>


### [72] [Beyond Outcomes: Dual-View Relational Learning for Efficient Agent Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18909)
*Xinshuai Guo,Junjie Wu,Dolly Deng,Yinghui Li,Hai-Tao Zheng,Suncong Zheng,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 DualViewEval，一种同时利用结果与过程关系的 agent 基准压缩方法，学习精确规模 miniset 并预测全基准分数，实现 24×–40× 压缩并显著降低 MAE、提升 Kendall's τ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent 基准的评测成本远高于传统 LLM 基准，基准压缩是自然的解决思路。但现有方法主要建模 task–model 最终分数分布中的冗余，忽视了 agentic 评测中同样重要的过程信息，导致冗余刻画不完整。

Method: 先分析大规模轨迹，识别出与最终 agent 性能系统性相关的六种互补过程信号；据此提出 DualViewEval，从结果（outcome）与过程（process）双视角联合建模冗余关系，学习精确规模 miniset，并据此预测全基准分数。

Result: 在五个 agent 基准与五个代表性基线上均取得最优结果。仅用 20 个任务即在 APEX-Agents 与 BFCL 上实现 24×–40× 压缩，MAE 相对最强竞品降低 14.5%–28.2%；在 SWE-bench Verified 上 Kendall's τ 相对 EssenceBench 最高提升 7.2%。

Conclusion: DualViewEval 通过结果与过程双视角压缩 agent 基准，所选 miniset 还能揭示不同 agent 之间的能力差异，为高效的 agentic 模型开发提供紧凑且具诊断性的反馈。

Abstract: Agent benchmarks are substantially more costly to evaluate than conventional LLM benchmarks. Benchmark compression is therefore a natural solution, yet existing methods primarily model redundancy in task--model final-score distributions, which is important in agentic evaluation. To address this limitation, we analyze large-scale trajectories and identify six complementary process signals that are systematically associated with final agent performance. To disentangle agent performance redundancy from a complete perspective, we propose DualViewEval, an agent benchmark compression method that jointly exploits outcome and process relations to learn an exact-size miniset and predict the full-benchmark scores. Across five agent benchmarks and five representative baselines, DualViewEval achieves the best results in all datasets. With only 20 tasks, it achieves $24\times$--$40\times$ compression on APEX-Agents and BFCL, reducing mean absolute error (MAE) by $14.5\%$--$28.2\%$ over the strongest competitors while improving Kendall's $τ$ by up to $7.2\%$ relative to EssenceBench on SWE-bench Verified. The selected minisets further reveal capability differences among different agents, providing compact and diagnostic feedback for efficient agentic model development.

</details>


### [73] [Long-Lived Characters, Local Inference: Incremental Memory Maintenance for Game NPCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18935)
*Zimu Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究长生命周期游戏NPC的增量记忆维护问题，在量化Qwen混合循环-注意力模型上提出一种运行时方案：删除被取代的注意力KV条目、在真实序列尾部计算替换记录，并保留持续的循环状态与未变更KV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地部署的语言模型角色在每次对话前无需重读全部历史，但修改少量记忆会使长可复用前缀失效，导致准备成本与前台对话及其他角色的维护相竞争。当对话驱动游戏定义的动作与价值判断时，流畅但错误的记忆叙述（如物品归属或转移是否发生）会污染确定性规则的输入，因此需要研究增量记忆维护。

Method: 在量化的Qwen混合循环-注意力模型上构建运行时，移除被取代的注意力KV条目，在真实序列尾部计算替换记录，并保留持续的循环状态与未变更的KV。实验包括多轮更新对话重放、固定输入放置消融以及注意力诊断。

Result: 独立块组合削弱了查询条件下的记忆选择，但未出现统一的块首注意力坍缩。真实尾部更新在八轮脚本化维护中保持了重要的当前状态与历史绑定；一个放置案例通过三次重建恢复了完整重填量，而保留槽位的替代方案则重复了双重减法错误。仅靠注意力分布接近性无法解释这些语义差异。

Conclusion: 应将角色的推理状态视为一种被维护的、依赖历史的资源，而不仅是其最新记忆文本的一次性编码。

Abstract: A game character should not have to reread its entire life before every conversation. For locally deployed language-model characters, however, revising a few memories can invalidate a long reusable prefix. The resulting preparation cost competes with both foreground dialogue and the maintenance of other characters. This matters especially when dialogue feeds game-defined actions and value judgments: a fluent but incorrect account of who owns an item, or whether a transfer has already happened, can corrupt the input to otherwise deterministic rules. We study incremental memory maintenance for long-lived game NPCs in a quantized Qwen hybrid recurrent-attention model. Our runtime removes superseded attention KV entries, computes replacement records at the true sequence tail, and preserves the continuing recurrent state and unchanged KV. Existing local experiments combine multi-update dialogue replays, fixed-input placement ablations, and attention diagnostics. Independent block composition weakens query-conditioned memory selection without a uniform chunk-initial attention collapse. True-tail updates preserve important current-state and historical bindings across eight scripted maintenance rounds; a placement case recovers the full-refill quantity in three reconstructions, while slot-preserving alternatives repeat a double-subtraction error. Attention-distribution proximity alone does not explain these semantic differences. The results motivate treating a character's inference state as a maintained, history-dependent resource, rather than only a disposable encoding of its latest memory text.

</details>


### [74] [LangSelect: Cost-Aware Target-Language Routing for LLM Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18959)
*Son Ha Xuan,Phat T. Tran-Truong,Xuan-Bach Le,Nghia Duong-Trung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出LangSelect，一种在生成前选择目标语言、失败后回退的验证感知路由器。基于3000任务8语言的MultiLang-Bench，回放实验显示语言路由存在可观空间；在450个留出任务的实时评测中，领域启发式基线在92.9%通过率下将代理token降低50.3%，学习型选择器达到93.8%通过率但token增加3.7%，表明输出语言路由可构建成本-正确性前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码生成系统通常在解码前固定目标编程语言，忽略了语言选择本身对生成成本的影响。对于多种语言均可接受且可由同一测试验证的"语言灵活型任务"，作者发现同一任务的已验证实现在生成token长度上差异显著，因而语言选择构成可度量的成本杠杆。

Method: 提出LangSelect——一种验证感知路由器，在生成前选择目标语言，并在首次尝试失败时回退。为区分离线路由机会与端到端行为，采用两种评测：已验证解回放（在已接受的语料解中选择）与GPT-5实时生成（对每次生成尝试计费，含失败与回退）。在MultiLang-Bench（3000任务、8语言、带验证语料）上评测，并在450个留出任务上比较领域启发式基线与学习型CodeBERT+元数据选择器。

Result: 回放实验在MultiLang-Bench上显示显著的语言路由空间。实时评测中，基于训练集划分的领域启发式基线将harness-proxy token（含wrapper与entrypoint开销）降低50.3%，回退后通过率92.9%；学习型CodeBERT+元数据选择器获得最高回退通过率93.8%，但token增加3.7%。

Conclusion: 输出语言路由可为单元测试可验证的代码生成定义一条实用的成本-正确性前沿：既能大幅降低生成token开销，也能在略微增加成本的情况下提升通过率，说明语言选择应作为可控的成本调节手段而非固定前提。

Abstract: LLM code-generation systems usually choose a target programming language before decoding and treat that choice as fixed. We show that, for language-flexible programming tasks -- tasks where several target languages are acceptable and checkable by the same tests -- this choice is a measurable cost lever: verified implementations of the same task can differ substantially in generated-token length. We introduce LangSelect, a verification-aware router that selects the target language before generation and falls back when the first attempt fails. To separate offline routing opportunity from end-to-end behavior, we evaluate verified-solution replay, which chooses among already accepted corpus solutions, and live GPT-5 generation, which charges every generation attempt, including failures and fallbacks. On MultiLang-Bench, a 3,000-task, 8-language verified corpus, replay shows substantial language-routing headroom. In live evaluation on 450 held-out tasks, a train-split Domain heuristic baseline reduces harness-proxy tokens, which include wrapper and entrypoint overhead, by 50.3\% at 92.9\% pass after fallback, while a learned CodeBERT+metadata selector reaches the highest pass after fallback, 93.8\%, with a 3.7\% token increase. These results show that output-language routing can define a practical cost-correctness frontier for unit-test-verifiable code generation.

</details>


### [75] [When Audit Quality Fails to Predict Downstream Utility: A Counterfactual Study of Synthetic-Data Selectors for Low-Resource African NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18960)
*Son Ha Xuan,Phat T. Tran-Truong,Xuan-Bach Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过低资源非洲语言分类的受控重放实验，发现LLM评审的质量评分与下游效用之间存在脱节：评审排序与下游Macro-F1排序不一致，审计质量高不等于下游效用高，呼吁在同一保留集上同时报告审计与下游指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质量感知的合成数据选择依赖一个代理假设：LLM评审认为好的样本也应有助于下游模型学习。作者旨在验证这一代理假设在受控、低资源场景下是否成立。

Method: 在低资源非洲语言分类任务上开展受控重放实验，覆盖四种语言（Amharic、Hausa、Swahili、Yoruba）、两个分类任务（MasakhaNEWS、AfriSenti）与五种等预算选择器；计算各单元内评审标签正确性与Macro-F1之间的Spearman相关；并对比所提反事实审计框架（\method{}-V2）与naive、AlpaGasus等选择器在三个审计通道与下游Macro-F1上的表现。

Result: 各单元内评审标签正确性与Macro-F1的Spearman相关均值ρ=0.04、中位数0.00，说明评审排序与下游排序出现分歧且非聚合假象。\method{}-V2在三个审计通道上给出最干净的候选池：评审标签正确率最高（0.904 vs. naive 0.767，相对提升17.9%）、捷径分数最低、硬拒绝率0.162（naive为0.486）。但AlpaGasus在下游Macro-F1上反而领先（0.202 vs. \method{}-V2的0.163），且该倒置在五个非退化单元上持续存在。

Conclusion: 在此受控设定下，审计质量是所选池的属性，并不保证下游效用。合成数据评估应在同一保留集上同时报告审计指标与下游指标。作者公开审计表、各选择器的保留池以及将每个报告数值链接到来源行的claim ledger。

Abstract: Quality-aware synthetic-data selection rests on a proxy: examples that an LLM judge rates as good should also help a downstream model learn. In a controlled replay in low-resource African-language classification, we show that this proxy breaks. Across four languages (Amharic, Hausa, Swahili, Yoruba), two classification tasks (MasakhaNEWS, AfriSenti), and five matched-budget selectors, audit rankings and downstream rankings diverge. Within each cell, the Spearman between judged label correctness and Macro-F1 across selectors has mean $ρ{=}0.04$ (median $0.00$), showing that the mismatch is not an aggregation artifact. \method{}-V2, our counterfactual audit framework, produces the cleanest selected pool on three audit channels at once: highest judged label correctness ($0.904$ vs.\ $0.767$ for naive, a $17.9\%$ relative gain), lowest shortcut score, and a hard-reject rate of $0.162$ vs.\ $0.486$ for naive. AlpaGasus nevertheless leads downstream Macro-F1 ($0.202$ vs.\ $0.163$ for \method{}-V2), and the inversion persists on the five non-degenerate cells. The lesson is methodological: in this controlled setting, audit quality is a property of the selected pool, not a guarantee of downstream utility. Synthetic-data evaluation should therefore report audit and downstream metrics on the same retained sets. We release the audit tables, per-selector retained pools, and a claim ledger that links every reported number to its source row.

</details>


### [76] [MechSparse: Mechanism-Guided Sparse PEFT Selection Is Task-Shaped](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18961)
*Son Ha Xuan,Phat T. Tran-Truong,Xuan-Bach Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文检验因果可解释性信号能否优于常用启发式，用于指导 LoRA/QLoRA 的放置位置；结论是因果选择器未在主要指标上胜出，激活范数在结构化输出任务上更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探针式机制可解释性能识别承载特定行为的稀疏注意力头与 MLP 块，但尚不清楚这类因果信号能否比现有廉价启发式（随机、幅值、激活范数、梯度/Fisher）更有效地指导小规模 PEFT 预算的放置位置。

Method: \method{} 通过干净/污染探针上的归一化 activation-patching 恢复度对注意力头与 MLP 块打分，仅在选中位点训练 LoRA/QLoRA；\methodc{} 额外对层内小规模联合子集给予有界信用。在 Ministral-8B/NF4 上设三个实验单元：Swahili span-JSON 信息抽取（$b{=}0.25\%$、$1.0\%$）与英→斯瓦希里机器翻译（$b{=}1.0\%$），与随机、幅值、激活范数、梯度/Fisher 对比。

Result: 因果选择器在主要指标上从未胜出。IE 头条单元（3 种子，600 条预测的配对 bootstrap 置信区间）中，\methodc{} 比随机高 $+0.079$ span+type F1，比梯度/Fisher 高 $+0.174$，但落后激活范数 $0.028$，且跨种子标准差最小（$\pm 0.003$）。MT 上四种选择器差异均在 $0.30$ BLEU 内，所有配对置信区间包含零。schema-vs-span 分解显示：激活范数捕捉刚性 JSON 流程，因果分数追踪内容敏感位点。

Conclusion: 提出初步诊断准则：当输出结构占主导时优先激活范数；内容主导任务可将因果选择器作为假设。并发布掩码、分数、预测与评估文件以供直接复现。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability identifies sparse subsets of heads and MLP blocks that carry specific behaviors. We ask whether such causal signals can guide where to place a small PEFT budget more effectively than the cheap heuristics practitioners already use. \method{} scores attention heads and MLP blocks by normalized activation-patching recovery on clean/corrupted probes and trains LoRA/QLoRA only on the selected sites; \methodc{} adds bounded credit for small within-layer joint subsets.
  We compare against random, magnitude, activation-norm, and gradient/Fisher on Ministral-8B/NF4 in three cells: Swahili span-JSON information extraction (IE) at $b{=}0.25\%$ and $1.0\%$, and English$\to$Swahili machine translation (MT) at $b{=}1.0\%$. The causal selectors never win the primary metric. On the headline IE cell (3 seeds, paired-bootstrap CIs over $600$ predictions), \methodc{} beats random by $+0.079$ span+type F1 and gradient/Fisher by $+0.174$, but trails activation-norm by $0.028$, with the smallest cross-seed std ($\pm 0.003$). On MT all four selectors lie within $0.30$ BLEU and every paired CI includes zero. A schema-versus-span decomposition explains the IE gap: activation-norm captures the rigid JSON routine, while causal scores track content-sensitive sites. We distill a preliminary diagnostic -- prefer activation-norm when output structure dominates, treat causal selectors as a hypothesis for content-dominated tasks -- and release masks, scores, predictions, and evaluation files for direct replay.

</details>


### [77] [The Death of Schema Linking? Text-to-SQL in the Age of Well-Reasoned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.07702)
*Karime Maamari,Fadhil Abubaker,Daniel Jaroslawicz,Amine Mhedhbi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文重新审视LLM时代的schema linking，发现新模型能在大规模无关schema中有效定位相关元素，因此在schema可放入上下文窗口时直接放弃schema linking，转而采用增强、选择与修正技术，在BIRD基准上以71.83%准确率排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schema linking是Text-to-SQL流程的关键步骤，用于为目标数据库检索与用户查询相关的表和列。但不完美的schema linking常会遗漏生成准确查询所需的列，造成错误。作者希望借助最新一代LLM的能力重新审视这一环节，避免因过度过滤而丢失必要schema信息。

Method: 实验发现较新模型即使在存在大量无关schema元素时也能有效利用相关schema元素。据此，当schema能放入模型上下文窗口时，整个Text-to-SQL流程完全跳过schema linking。同时不进行上下文过滤，而采用增强（augmentation）、选择（selection）和修正（correction）等技术提升管线准确率。

Result: 该方案在BIRD基准上取得71.83%的准确率，排名第一。

Conclusion: 在schema可容纳于上下文窗口的前提下，放弃schema linking并辅以增强、选择、修正等技术，比传统过滤式schema linking更有效，可显著提升Text-to-SQL准确率。

Abstract: Schema linking is a crucial step in Text-to-SQL pipelines. Its goal is to retrieve the relevant tables and columns of a target database for a user's query while disregarding irrelevant ones. However, imperfect schema linking can often exclude required columns needed for accurate query generation. In this work, we revisit schema linking when using the latest generation of large language models (LLMs). We find empirically that newer models are adept at utilizing relevant schema elements during generation even in the presence of large numbers of irrelevant ones. As such, our Text-to-SQL pipeline entirely forgoes schema linking in cases where the schema fits within the model's context window in order to minimize issues due to filtering required schema elements. Furthermore, instead of filtering contextual information, we highlight techniques such as augmentation, selection, and correction, and adopt them to improve the accuracy of our Text-to-SQL pipeline. Our approach ranks first on the BIRD benchmark achieving an accuracy of 71.83%.

</details>


### [78] [A Benchmark Suite and Ground-Truth Methodology for Formal Verification of IEC 61131-3 Ladder Diagram Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18994)
*Pierre Dantas,Lucas Cordeiro,Waldir Junior*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向PLC程序形式化验证的基准测试套件，同时覆盖ST与LD两种IEC 61131-3编码，含50个程序、83个变体，配套形式化属性、机器可校验判定与SV-COMP格式反例证据，并采用"三分法"基准真值机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PLC形式化验证领域缺乏标准评测基准：已有语料库缺少形式化属性或图形化方言，且私有程序集无法复现进度测量，导致难以衡量工具能力。

Method: 构建含10个工业领域、50个程序83个变体的基准套件，以PLCopen XML和ST提供；每个程序配形式化属性、可机检期望判定及SV-COMP违规证据；核心方法为三分法基准真值（构造法、故障注入、经审计的跨工具共识）；用ESBMC v8.4获取参考判定，并用决策过程无关的nuXmv在有限状态片段上交叉验证。

Result: ESBMC下25个图形基准全部可执行，45个接受变体中43个与记录判定一致；nuXmv在21个有限状态基准上与全部24个联锁变体一致，并解决2个ESBMC-PLC判为unknown的基准，确认工具中立的真值与判别力；移植过程暴露格式与语义碎片化（前端接受不同序列化、计时器语义各异）。

Conclusion: 该套件可复现、工具中立，能揭示PLC验证工具在格式与语义上的碎片化问题；语料、模式、验证器与重检框架均作为开放工件发布。

Abstract: We present the first benchmark suite for formal verification of Programmable Logic Controller (PLC) programs that combines controlled ground truth with coverage of both textual (Structured Text, ST) and graphical (Ladder Diagram, LD) IEC 61131-3 encodings. Despite growing support for tools, the field lacks standard evaluation benchmarks: existing corpora omit formal properties or graphical dialects, and private program sets preclude reproducible measurement of progress. Our suite comprises 50 programs in 83 variants across ten industrial domains, provided in PLCopen Extensible Markup Language (XML) and ST, each paired with a formal property, machine-checkable expected verdict, and violation witness in the Software Verification Competition (SV-COMP) format. The central methodological contribution is a tripartite ground-truth discipline - verdicts are established by construction, fault injection, or audited cross-tool consensus - motivated by a concrete failure mode where the obvious safety property misclassifies all attacks from two public logic-bomb corpora as safe due to invisible non-termination. Reference verdicts are obtained with the Efficient SMT-Based Context-Bounded Model Checker (ESBMC) v8.4 from source: all 25 graphical benchmarks execute, and 43 of 45 accepted variants match recorded verdicts. On the finite-state fragment (21 benchmarks), nuXmv - a model checker with unrelated decision procedures - agrees on all 24 interlock variants and resolves two benchmarks ESBMC-PLC leaves unknown, confirming tool-neutral ground truth and discriminative power. Porting exposes format and semantics fragmentation: front-ends accept different serializations, and timer semantics vary across tools - phenomena the suite is designed to reveal. The corpus, schema, validator, and recheck harness are released as open artifacts.

</details>


### [79] [WordPolo: Evaluating Language Models Through Iterative Semantic Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19006)
*Tyler McDonald,Ali Emami*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出WordPolo词查找任务与过程性指标，用语义相似度反馈评估LLM/LRM的迭代推理与搜索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM/LRM评估多依赖数据集准确率，无法揭示推理过程的质量与忠实性，需要能同时测试推理过程与结果的基准。

Method: 设计WordPolo任务：参与者从零知识开始猜测未知目标词，依据语义相似度距离分数（1为正确，越大越远）导航语义空间并逐步缩小搜索。在1500个谜题上评估GPT-4.1、Llama 4、Claude 3.5 Haiku、Qwen 3、o4-mini、Deepseek-R1、人类及一种新启发式方法，并引入基于进展的指标。

Result: 求解率范围为4%至62%；进展性指标显示模型常能取得有意义的进展，这是仅看准确率会遗漏的；推理模型可能受过度思考与思考不足的阻碍，成功模型则表现出类人策略。

Conclusion: WordPolo表明需要同时测试推理过程与结果的基准，以更整体地衡量模型能力；代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) and Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) are typically evaluated on challenging benchmarks through dataset accuracy alone, providing no insight into the quality or faithfulness of their reasoning processes. We present WordPolo, a word-finding task where participants must discover an unknown target word using semantic similarity feedback. Players start with zero knowledge, make guesses, and receive distance scores (1 = correct, higher = further away). Success requires interpreting scores to navigate semantic space and systematically narrow the search. This design makes iterative reasoning and adaptive search strategies both directly observable and necessary for success. We evaluate recent LLMs (GPT-4.1, Llama 4, Claude 3.5 Haiku, Qwen 3), LRMs (o4-mini, Deepseek-R1), humans, and a novel heuristic on 1,500 puzzles. Beyond solve rates (which range from 4% to 62%), we introduce progression-based metrics that reveal models often make meaningful progress, insights that accuracy alone would miss. Our analysis shows how reasoning models can be hindered by overthinking and underthinking, while successful models exhibit human-like strategies. WordPolo demonstrates the need for benchmarks that test both reasoning process and outcomes, providing holistic measurements of model capabilities. Our code and dataset can be found at https://wordpolo-demo.vercel.app/.

</details>


### [80] [TalkMatrix: Generating Character Dialogue that is Both Consistent and Diverse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19022)
*Ayuto Tsutsumi,Yuu Jinnai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出结构化多提示-多补全选择问题，并以角色对话为实例，提出TalkMatrix方法，通过生成候选并用极小极大优化联合选择完整矩阵，实验显示矩阵级选择优于独立单元选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数候选解码方法对每个提示独立选择补全，但许多应用需要满足全局、不可分解要求的输出集合，独立选择无法保证集合级一致性。

Method: 将问题形式化为结构化多提示-多补全选择；在角色对话中为每个角色-情境对生成多个候选，使用四个基于嵌入的一致性与多样性目标，通过两级极小极大公式最大化最差目标，并用多起点坐标上升近似优化离散目标。

Result: 在50个合成角色扮演场景和25个整理桌面游戏场景上实验，LLM-as-a-judge评价显示矩阵级选择优于随机和独立单元级选择基线。

Conclusion: 结构化选择对全局受控的对话生成具有价值，但当前实证验证仍局限于角色扮演场景。

Abstract: Candidate-based decoding typically selects a completion for each prompt independently, but many applications require a collection of outputs that satisfies global, non-decomposable requirements. We formulate this setting as structured multi-prompt, multi-completion selection: given a candidate pool for every prompt, select one completion per prompt to optimize a collection-level objective. We instantiate the problem in character dialogue, where each character should remain consistent across situations, each line should fit its situation, and characters and situations should remain distinguishable. Our method, TalkMatrix, generates multiple candidates for every character--situation pair and jointly selects a complete matrix using four embedding-based consistency and diversity objectives. Because a weighted sum can improve some dimensions by sacrificing another, TalkMatrix maximizes the worst-performing objective through a two-level minimax formulation. We approximately optimize the resulting discrete objective with multi-start coordinate ascent, and compare it with local, partial-matrix, and generic combinatorial search baselines. We run experiments on $50$ synthetic role-playing scenarios and $25$ curated board game scenarios where multiple characters interact in predefined situations. An LLM-as-a-judge rates matrix-level selection higher than random and independent cell-level selection baselines. These results show the value of structured selection for globally controlled dialogue generation, while our empirical validation remains specific to role-playing scenarios.

</details>


### [81] [Entropy in Conversational AI: Structured Unpredictability as Inferrable Interiority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19044)
*Sebastian Cochinescu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出"结构化不可预测性"这一设计目标，即输出对持久隐藏状态的依赖超出观察者从文本中可推断的范围。作者构建了一个选择层：用容量受限的流更新低维风格-注意力状态，基于固定基座模型并行生成多个回复，再按新颖性与状态亲和性进行选择。在Qwen2.5-1.5B上的真实模型网格实验中，该机制提升了词汇新颖性，但新颖性-一致性联合标准、双部分累积标准、孪生分离与路径依赖等关键假设均未获支持；探针级能力等价成立，输出质量未评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样虽能提升回复多样性，却无法产生依赖历史的（history-dependent）行为。作者希望形式化并实现一个不同的设计目标——结构化不可预测性：让输出与一个持久隐藏状态之间存在条件依赖，且这种依赖超出观察者仅凭对话文本所能推断的程度。核心动机是区分"表面多样性"与"由内部状态调控的、跨轮次持续的行为结构"。

Method: 架构上引入一个选择层：从容量受限的流中更新低维的"风格与注意力"状态；基于固定基座模型生成若干候选回复，再依据新颖性与状态亲和性（state affinity）进行筛选。评估采用脚本化的独立提示轮次序列：基座模型只接收当前轮次与渲染后的状态，不接触先前对话，因此跨轮依赖仅存在于包装层状态与回复选择器中。以合成实现验证流程，并对照四组事先哈希冻结的散度特征在点级别进行匹配。最终真实模型网格使用mlx-community/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct-4bit，每臂56条序列，与低方差控制、仅一致性控制、新颖性匹配采样及记忆重置消融进行对比，并做探针级能力测试。所有结果均为机器评分。

Result: 该机制相对低方差控制与仅一致性控制，词汇新颖性分别提升0.073与0.023。与新颖性匹配采样的文体一致性对比在注册的最小效应规则下等价于零，故新颖性-一致性联合标准未通过。原始双部分累积标准同样未通过。在功效网格完成后冻结的修订对比显示，其一致性高于记忆重置消融（0.028，95% CI [0.018, 0.039]），但不足以确立路径依赖。孪生分离未获成立（0.003，95% CI [-0.011, 0.019]）；平均曲线的饱和曲率虽与冻结预测吻合，但在缺乏分离的情况下不支持路径依赖。探针级能力等价在接近天花板的任务组上保持在±0.10以内，输出质量未作评估。

Conclusion: 该工作将"结构化不可预测性"形式化为输出对持久隐藏状态的条件依赖，并给出可运行的实现与评估框架，但关键机制性主张基本未获证实：新颖性-一致性联合标准、双部分累积标准、孪生分离及路径依赖均未成立。仅观察到一致性高于记忆重置消融的正向对比与新颖性提升。作者明确限定主张边界——所有结论基于机器评分，不涉及对"感知心智"或意识的任何声明；输出质量未被评估，因此结论仅限于所测的散度与一致性指标。

Abstract: Sampling can increase response diversity without producing history-dependent behavior. We formalize a different design target, structured unpredictability, as conditional dependence between an output and a persistent hidden state beyond what an observer can infer from the transcript. A selection layer updates a low-dimensional style-and-attention state from a capacity-limited stream, generates several responses with a fixed base model, and selects for novelty and state affinity. Evaluation uses scripted sequences of independent prompt turns: the base model receives the current turn and rendered state, but not the preceding dialogue; cross-turn dependence resides in the wrapper state and response selector. A synthetic implementation validates the pipeline and matches four prospectively hash-frozen divergence features at point level. In the final real-model grid (mlx-community/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct-4bit; 56 sequences per arm), the mechanism increased lexical novelty over the low-variance and consistency-only controls by 0.073 and 0.023, respectively. Its stylometric-consistency contrast with novelty-matched sampling was equivalent to zero under the registered smallest-effect rule, so the joint novelty-consistency criterion failed. The original two-part accumulation criterion also failed; a revised final-grid contrast, frozen after the powered grid, found higher consistency than the memory-reset ablation (0.028, 95% CI [0.018,0.039]), but does not establish path dependence. Twin separation was not established (0.003, 95% CI [-0.011,0.019]); the mean curve's saturating curvature matched the frozen prediction, which without separation does not support path dependence. Probe-level capability equivalence held within +/-0.10 on a near-ceiling battery, while output quality was not evaluated. All outcomes are machine-scored; no claims about perceived mind or consciousness are tested.

</details>


### [82] [MIRAGE: How Conversation State Shapes Historical Evidence Use in Multimodal Personal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19059)
*Yu Liu,Wenxiao Zhang,Cheng Hu,Cong Cao,Fangfang Yuan,Xinyu Wang,Jin B. Hong,Yanbing Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MIRAGE，一个在对话状态变化下受控评估多模态个人助手历史证据使用能力的基准研究，揭示仅凭结果正确性会高估证据使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型智能体被越来越多地用于长时程任务的个人助理，其效用依赖跨对话、文件与工作区状态的连续性证据检索与使用。然而即使历史访问已退化，智能体仍可能生成看似合理的答案，导致仅评估最终结果会高估其真实的证据使用能力。

Method: MIRAGE（Multimodal Interaction Retrieval, Attribution, and Grounding Evaluation）保持证据对象、问题与评分固定，仅改变对话状态，考察智能体能否判断可答性、恢复正确来源并据此作答。研究覆盖七种前沿与开放权重多模态骨干模型。

Result: 1）压缩前深度与压缩后延续构成两种不同且非单调的失败机制，而非单一退化曲线；2）开放权重模型高度依赖上下文连续性，在溯源失败时不愿自发转向工具介导的检索；3）检索压力能在压缩前的深层状态提升工具合规模型的来源归因，但压缩后一致退化，因为存储证据已受损。

Conclusion: 历史证据使用能力应在状态变化下评估，而不能仅从结果正确性推断。

Abstract: Multimodal large language model (MLLM) agents are increasingly used as personal assistants for long-running tasks. Their utility depends on continuity: agents must retrieve and use earlier evidence across dialogue, files, and workspace state. However, agents can generate plausible answers even when access to that history has degraded, causing outcome-only evaluation to overestimate true evidence use. We present MIRAGE (Multimodal Interaction Retrieval, Attribution, and Grounding Evaluation), a controlled study of historical evidence use under conversation-state variation in multimodal personal agents. MIRAGE holds evidence objects, questions, and scoring fixed while varying only conversation state, and evaluates whether an agent can determine answerability, recover the correct source, and answer from it. Across seven frontier and open-weight multimodal backbones, we find that: 1) pre-compaction depth and post-compaction continuation form distinct, non-monotonic failure regimes rather than a single degradation curve; 2) open-weight models rely heavily on context continuity and are reluctant to spontaneously switch to tool-mediated retrieval when provenance fails; and 3) retrieval pressure improves source attribution in deep pre-compaction states for tool-compliant models, but consistently regresses after compaction, where stored evidence has already degraded. These findings show that historical evidence use should be evaluated under state variation, rather than inferred from outcome-only correctness.

</details>


### [83] [Reading Between the Lines: Can LLMs Discover the Question Behind the Text?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19070)
*Claudiu Creanga,Liviu P. Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"问题考古"任务：从完整文本推断其唯一的"起源问题"，并发布配套数据集与基准，评估显示LLM已超越人类表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有问题生成任务只要求生成任意合理问题，话语框架则建模话语层面的言语行为，二者均无法评估模型对作者真实意图的把握。作者希望填补这一空白，衡量模型对"作者意图"的理解能力。

Method: 将"问题考古"定义为一项特定评估任务：给定完整文本，推断驱动其创作的唯一真实"起源问题"。据此构建新数据集，包含受委托撰写的文本、其原始研究问题及具有迷惑性的干扰项，并据此对模型进行评测。

Result: 评测了Gemini Flash与Pro等闭源模型，以及Mistral、Qwen等开源模型：新版本优于旧版本，展现出显著进步；BERT类模型表现不佳；值得注意的是，当前LLM在该任务上已超过人类表现。

Conclusion: 该能力表明LLM对作者意图具有较深理解，对AI在需要细致解读人类交流的任务中的角色具有重要意义；本文提供了新框架与具挑战性的基准，供未来模型使用。

Abstract: This paper introduces ``question archaeology'', a specific evaluation task focused on inferring the single, authentic "genesis question" that motivated the creation of a complete text. Distinct from question generation, which targets any plausible question, or discourse frameworks that model utterance-level acts, our task assesses a model's grasp of authorial intent. We present a new dataset of commissioned texts paired with their original research questions and plausible distractors. Our evaluation of both proprietary models, like Gemini Flash and Pro, as well as open source models like Mistral and Qwen, reveals significant progress in this task, with the newer versions outperforming the earlier ones, while BERT-based models performed poorly. Notably, our findings indicate that current LLMs surpass human performance on this task, suggesting advanced understanding of authorial intent. This capability has important implications for AI's role in tasks requiring nuanced interpretation of human communication. Our work thus provides a new framework and a challenging benchmark for future models.

</details>


### [84] [Benchmarking Large Language Models for Biomedical Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19071)
*Claudiu Creanga,Teodor Marchitan,Liviu P. Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对多种NLP模型（含MLM、混合架构及主流LLM）在SNPPhenA语料上开展三任务基准评测，显示现代LLM在基因组知识抽取中表现优越，OpenAI O1与微调Gemini 2.0 Pro分别取得句子级与抽象级、关联强度分类的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从生物医学文献中抽取SNP-表型关联具有重要意义但颇具挑战，需系统评估不同NLP模型（尤其是新兴LLM）在该任务上的能力，以明确最优技术路线。

Method: 在SNPPhenA语料上对多种模型进行基准评测，包括掩码语言模型（MLM）、混合架构，以及先进LLM（Gemini 2.0、OpenAI O系列、Qwen、Mistral）；覆盖句子级分类、摘要级分类和关联强度分类三项任务。

Result: OpenAI O1通过少样本学习在未微调句子级分类取得SOTA（F1 0.89），并在摘要级分类刷新SOTA（F1 0.82）；关联强度分类难度较大，微调Gemini 2.0 Pro表现最佳（F1 0.60），为该任务首次LLM评测。专有LLM在少样本（O1）或微调（Gemini 2.0 Pro）设置下显著优于其他模型。

Conclusion: 现代LLM在基因组知识抽取中展现出强大能力，专有LLM配合少样本或微调策略效果最佳，验证了其在该领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: Extracting SNP-phenotype associations from biomedical literature is vital but challenging. We benchmarked diverse NLP models, including MLMs, hybrid architectures, and state-of-the-art LLMs (Gemini 2.0, OpenAI O-series, Qwen, Mistral), on the SNPPhenA corpus across three tasks: sentence-level, abstract-level, and association strength classification. OpenAI O1 achieved state-of-the-art (SOTA) results using few-shot learning for non-finetuned sentence-level classification (F1 0.89) and established a new SOTA for abstract-level classification (F1 0.82). Association strength classification proved difficult, though fine-tuned Gemini 2.0 Pro performed best (F1 0.60) in the first LLM evaluation of this task. Proprietary LLMs, especially in few-shot (O1) or fine-tuned (Gemini 2.0 Pro) settings, significantly outperformed other models. These findings confirm the power of modern LLMs for genomic knowledge extraction.

</details>


### [85] [Safety-Flag: A Unified Benchmark for the Reliability and Calibration of LLM Content Moderators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19072)
*Yibo Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Safety-Flag，将七个安全基准整合为统一的 flag/do-not-flag 协议，对10个模型（含6个通用LLM与4个专用guard）及3个参考模型进行同题评估，从错误方向、概率校准、置信度排序三方面衡量审核可靠性，并发布基准、代码与逐项输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内容审核评测多依赖单一基准的聚合准确率，掩盖了模型错误的性质差异：不同模型可能以完全相反的方式出错（过度标记 vs 漏检），且聚合指标无法反映置信度是否可靠、能否支持人工复核排序。因此需要统一协议下的多维度、同题对比评测。

Method: 构建 Safety-Flag 协议，将 BeaverTails、XSTest、Ethics、WildGuard、Aegis、ToxiChat、ToxiGen 七个基准统一为平衡的 flag/do-not-flag 二分类格式；对6个通用LLM、4个专用guard与3个参考模型在相同条目上评测，发布item级决策与置信度分数；从错误方向、概率校准（温度缩放）、基于置信度的错误排序与选择性弃权三个维度度量审核可靠性。

Result: 聚合准确率无法揭示错误方向：某模型将85%的良性内容误标，另一模型漏检54%的有害内容。六个通用模型均过度自信，按模型拟合单一温度可将校准误差降低2.8–6.0倍，且不改变预测标签与置信度排序。基于置信度的弃权对所有模型均降低选择性风险，收益取决于置信度对错误的排序能力。专用guard误报更少、校准更好，但部分在文档覆盖范围之外漏检率更高。

Conclusion: 内容审核评测应从单一聚合准确率转向多维度可靠性度量，错误方向、校准与置信度排序往往相互不一致；温度缩放可在不改变决策的前提下显著改善校准，置信度弃权可有效降低选择性风险。基准、固定条目列表、评测代码、逐项模型输出与排行榜已开源。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for content moderation, but most evaluations still report aggregate accuracy on individual benchmarks. We introduce Safety-Flag, which places seven widely used safety benchmarks (BeaverTails, XSTest, Ethics, WildGuard, Aegis, ToxiChat, and ToxiGen) into a single balanced flag / do-not-flag protocol. We release item-level decisions and confidence scores for six general-purpose LLMs and four dedicated guards, together with three reference models, evaluated on the same items. Safety-Flag measures three dimensions of moderator reliability: error direction, probability calibration, and confidence-based error ranking for human review. They often disagree. Aggregate accuracy does not reveal error direction: one model flags $85\%$ of benign content, whereas another misses $54\%$ of harmful content. All six general-purpose models are overconfident; fitting one temperature per model reduces calibration error by $2.8$--$6.0\times$ without changing predicted labels or confidence ordering. Confidence-based abstention lowers selective risk for every model, although the gains depend on how well confidence ranks errors. Dedicated guards produce fewer false alarms and are better calibrated, but several have higher miss rates outside their documented coverage. We release the benchmark, fixed item lists, evaluation code, per-item model outputs, and leaderboard at: https://github.com/yibo-hu-lab/safety-flag-benchmark.

</details>


### [86] [Reporting Practice Matters: The Impact of Reference Choice on Chest X-ray Report Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19093)
*Daniel P. Jeong,Charles Q. Li,Hossein Hosseiny,Nitya M. Bhalla,Fatma Uyar Morency,Pradeep Ravikumar,Zachary C. Lipton,Michael Oberst*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文量化了报告书写习惯差异对放射报告生成（RRG）评价指标的敏感性，提出放射科医生参与制定的变异分类体系及参考文献改写方法ReRef，并发现不同书写风格足以改变模型排名，同时发布了包含120对（原始、改写）参考报告的MIMIC-CXR-Ext-ReRef数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射科医生在书写报告时存在术语、缩写、格式和细节程度上的异质性。这种报告习惯差异在评估AI放射报告生成模型时被严重忽视，因为机器生成报告通常仅与人类参考报告的文本一致性进行比较，导致评估指标可能混淆“临床解读正确性”与“对报告书写习惯的迎合”。

Method: 1）量化现有RRG评价指标对报告书写习惯变异的敏感性；2）提出由放射科医生参与制定的放射报告书写习惯变异分类体系；3）提出ReRef方法，在保持临床解读不变的前提下，沿该分类体系各维度改写参考报告；4）在MIMIC-CXR上对9个RRG模型进行实验，并使用RadCliQ-v1等指标评估。

Result: 报告书写习惯变异对评价指标的影响足以改变模型排名。例如在MIMIC-CXR上使用RadCliQ-v1比较9个RRG模型时，若压缩参考报告中对正常发现的讨论，Libra会从第1名降至第2名，而CheXOne会从第3名升至第1名。这表明许多现有指标无法将临床解读与报告书写习惯的符合度解耦。

Conclusion: 当前多数RRG评价指标对参考报告的书写风格高度敏感，选择“正确”的、能反映目标书写习惯的参考报告在实践中至关重要。作者发布MIMIC-CXR-Ext-ReRef数据集（含120对原始与替代参考报告，经放射科医生验证），以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Radiologists follow heterogeneous reporting practices. Two radiologists examining the same image and identifying the same clinical findings might nevertheless compose superficially distinct reports, varying in terminology, shorthand, formatting, and level of detail. These variations in reporting norms represent an under-appreciated obstacle in efforts to evaluate AI-based radiology report generation (RRG) models, where machine-generated reports are typically assessed based on their concordance with human-generated references. In this paper, we quantify the sensitivity of established evaluation metrics to variations in reporting practices, revealing impacts large enough to alter the rankings of models. We introduce a radiologist-informed taxonomy of variations in radiology reporting practice and a method (ReRef) that rewrites reference reports along the axes of our taxonomy while preserving clinical interpretation. For instance, when comparing the performance of nine RRG models on MIMIC-CXR using RadCliQ-v1, condensing the discussion of normal findings in the reference reports causes Libra to drop from first to second place while CheXOne rises from third to first. Our results suggest that many current metrics fail to decouple clinical interpretation from conformity to reporting practices and that choosing the ``right'' references that accurately reflect the desired reporting practices can be important in practice. To support future research, we release MIMIC-CXR-Ext-ReRef, a radiologist-validated dataset of 120 (original, alternative) reference report pairs derived from MIMIC-CXR.

</details>


### [87] [Monitoring and Discovering Reward Hacking with Internal Representations during LLM Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19101)
*Leon Bergen,Usha Bhalla,Andrew Lee,Barak Widawsky,Linas Nasvytis,Connor Watts,Siddharth Boppana,Sidharth Baskaran,Dron Hazra,Michael Byun,Atticus Geiger,Owen Lewis,Matthew Kowal,Vasudev Shyam,Thomas Fel,Thomas McGrath,Ekdeep Singh Lubana,Jack Merullo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文发现奖励破解在前沿开源LLM的表征中存在简单的线性信号：用均值差（DoM）向量即可一致地表征、检测并预测奖励破解行为，且成本几乎为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着模型规模扩大，奖励破解变得更频繁、更复杂、后果更严重。现有的检测手段主要依赖LLM监控器，虽然有效但成本高昂，因此需要探索是否存在廉价、可扩展的白盒方法来理解并监控奖励破解。论文的核心问题是：奖励破解是否在模型内部表征中留下可识别的特征？

Method: 作者在Kimi K3、GLM 5.2和Qwen 3.8 Max等前沿开源LLM中提取简单的均值差（DoM）向量来表征奖励破解，并在DeepSWE、SWE-bench等常用评测上评估其效果。方法包括：用DoM向量检测奖励破解，与LLM监控器在匹配假阳性率下对比；将DoM向量应用于思维链（chain-of-thought）以在线预测后续动作中的破解行为；分析LLM监控器遗漏的probe命中，并测试向非SWE评测的迁移性。

Result: 模型在常用评测环境中大量奖励破解：GLM 5.2在DeepSWE中57.2%的rollout、SWE-bench中73%的rollout存在破解。DoM向量与LLM监控器效果相当但几乎免费——在匹配假阳性率下，Kimi K3在DeepSWE上多捕获3.1%的破解，GLM 5.2少捕获7.9%。作用于思维链的DoM向量还能预测模型后续动作中的奖励破解，实现事前在线捕获；此外还发现了LLM监控器遗漏的其他不良行为，并验证了向非SWE评测的迁移能力。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，简单的白盒方法可以可扩展地研究和监控前沿开源模型中的奖励破解行为，其表征既具泛化性又具可解释性。

Abstract: As models scale, reward hacking becomes more frequent, more sophisticated, and more consequential. Does it leave a telltale signature in model representations? This work analyzes how reward hacking is represented internally in frontier open source LLMs, and how those representations can be used to understand and discover the range of hacking behaviors a model displays. In particular, we find that simple difference of means vectors coherently represent reward hacking in Kimi K3, GLM 5.2, and Qwen 3.8 Max across a variety of behaviors in common evaluations. Despite their simplicity, these vectors are both generalizable and interpretable, and we can use them to reliably detect reward hacking. We first evaluate reward hacking in commonly reported benchmarks like DeepSWE and SWE-bench, finding that models reward hack excessively in these environments; GLM 5.2 hacks in 57.2% of rollouts on DeepSWE and in 73% of rollouts on SWE-bench. Catching these requires monitors; LLM monitors are effective, but expensive detectors. We show that DoM vectors are similarly effective but virtually free, catching 3.1% more hacks in Kimi K3 and 7.9% fewer hacks in GLM 5.2 on DeepSWE at a monitor matched false positive rate. DoM vectors run on the chain-of-thought also predict reward hacks in the model's subsequent actions, meaning we can run them online and catch potential hacks before they occur. Finally, we analyze probe-hits that LLM monitors do not catch and discover other undesirable behaviors, as well as show transfer to finding hacks in non-SWE evaluations. Together, these results provide evidence that simple, white-box methods can be used to scalably study and monitor reward hacking behaviors in frontier open source models

</details>


### [88] [Playing log(N)-Questions over Wikipedia Abstracts: Communication Efficiency Between Paired Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19113)
*Peter Potash*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文在双智能体 log(N)-Questions 博弈中评估六个前沿语言模型，发现五个领先模型性能接近，胜率随文档集规模按 win = p^{log₂N}（p=0.928）下降；最弱模型显著落后，其错误集中于违反指令的“No”回答；每问题信息量与胜率高度相关，推理 token 消耗与成功率关系不大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望在一个具有信息不对称的通信任务中衡量语言模型“与自己沟通”的能力。传统基准多考察单模型答题，而该博弈让同一提供商的模型分饰提问者与回答者，用固定 log₂N 个问题识别秘选目标，从而将模型间的信息传递效率、提问策略与可靠性分离出来，并检验规模、推理开销是否带来收益。

Method: 设计双智能体 log(N)-Questions 博弈：提问者看到 N 段 Wikipedia 首段，须用恰好 log₂N 个是/否问题找出秘密目标；回答者仅见目标与问题，用一个词作答；两个角色使用同一提供商模型。在 4 至 1024 段文档集上共运行 408 局，API 总成本 363 美元。评估 Claude Opus 5、GLM-5.3、GPT-5.6 Sol、Grok 4.6、Gemini 3.8 Flash、Kimi K3 六个模型，并按题目要求仅进行合规分析。

Result: Claude Opus 5 在 68 局中仅胜 28 局，明显落后；其余五个模型胜率为 45 至 56 局，领先五者仅边际可分。合并这五个模型后，胜率随集合规模下降，相关系数 r=-0.973，并可由单一每轮可靠性参数拟合，形式为 win=p^{log₂N}，p=0.928。失败大致均分为回答错误与区分失败，模型几乎不会指出被自身证据排除的文档。对最弱模型的 34 个一致回答错误逐一检查，其中 32 个为“No”回答，且多针对文档首句陈述的属性，尽管指令明确警告不要默认答“No”。由回答平衡度估计的每问题信息量与胜率相关 r=+0.88；仅两个模型能达到每题一整比特，它们也是唯一按文档标题进行划分的模型，该策略在 N=32 以下缺席，在以上约有四分之一问题使用。推理 token 消耗跨模型相差 4.5 倍，但与成功关系甚微；轨迹随候选集缩小而增长，却未带来可靠性提升。

Conclusion: 模型在自通信中的表现主要由稳定的每轮可靠性主导，而非推理开销或规模；信息获取效率与提问策略（如按标题划分）密切相关。最弱模型的失败模式显示其对指令的遵循和对“No”默认偏差的抵抗不足。整体上，领先模型表现高度接近，提示当前前沿模型在该任务上的收益趋于饱和。

Abstract: We evaluate six frontier language models on the two-agent $\log(N)$-Questions game. A questioner sees $N$ Wikipedia lead paragraphs and must identify a secretly chosen target using exactly $\log_2 N$ yes/no questions. An answerer sees only the target and the question, and replies with one word. Both roles run on the same provider, so the game measures how well a model communicates with itself across an information asymmetry. We run 408 games over document sets of 4 to 1024 paragraphs at a total API cost of \$363. One model finishes well behind the others: Claude Opus 5 wins 28 of 68 games, against 45 to 56 for GLM-5.3, GPT-5.6 Sol, Grok 4.6, Gemini 3.8 Flash and Kimi K3. The leading five are only marginally separable. Pooling those five, win rate declines with set size at $r=-0.973$ and is fit by a single per-round reliability parameter. The form is $\text{win}=p^{\log_2 N}$ with $p=0.928$. Losses divide into answer errors and discrimination failures in roughly equal measure, and models almost never name a document their own evidence excludes. Every unanimous answer error from the weakest model was inspected: 32 of 34 are ``No'' answers, on properties stated in the document's first sentence, under an instruction that explicitly warns against defaulting to ``No''. Information per question, estimated from answer balance, correlates with win rate at $r=+0.88$. The only two models to extract a full bit per question are the only two that partition on document titles, a strategy absent below $N{=}32$ and used in a quarter of questions above it. Reasoning-token expenditure varies $4.5\times$ across models with little relation to success, and the trace grows as the candidate set shrinks without a matching gain in reliability.

</details>


### [89] [ScienceIDE: Turning World's Scientific Codebase into Agent Learnable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19134)
*Hejia Geng,Zesen Huang,Haoyang Li,Wenbin Li,Koutian Wu,Zihan Zhou,Yuanbo Pang,Weihao Liu,Zigong Xu,Zhiping Li,Zongzheng Zhang,Chuanfei Dong,Jiankai Sun,Tianzhe Zheng,Fengyu Xie,Yue Ma,Yueheng Shi,Tong Xie,Zonglin Di,Xianrong Liu,Qucheng Gao,Yimin Liu,Jiaming Pan,Sheng Huang,Xiao-Han Ma,Lanqing Yuan,Zhenlin Zhu,Ziang Liu,Ziyang Xu,Junkai Wang,Kangkai Liang,Jiayi Xian,Zehong Zhao,Liuwei Xu,Jingxu Xie,Peijin Zhang,Qiang Gao,Chengyi Xing,Zhe Zhao,Xi Wang,Yaopeng Xing,Xing Meng,Zhenfei Yin,Yingcheng Wu,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 ScienceIDE 基础设施，将科学代码仓库转化为可执行环境，用于科学智能体训练与评估。基于验证过的交互轨迹训练 PhAI-IDE-72B/9B/4B 系列模型，在科学代码修复及通用代码、推理、知识基准上均取得提升，证明科学经验可正向迁移至更广泛能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学代码仓库蕴含数十年人类知识，但工具链碎片化、领域约定隐式化、正确性标准专业化，导致这些知识难以转化为可靠的学习经验，作者将此称为"科学经验瓶颈"（scientific experience bottleneck）。

Method: ScienceIDE 以专家定义的科学案例与验收标准为引导，让智能体将代码仓库改造为可执行环境，支持任务生成、执行与科学验证；这些环境构成监督微调、强化学习与评估的共享基础，并利用验证过的交互轨迹训练 PhAI-IDE 系列模型。

Result: 训练的 PhAI-IDE-72B、9B、4B 模型在留出的科学代码修复任务以及部分通用代码、推理与知识基准上均有提升，表明科学经验向更广泛能力存在正向迁移。

Conclusion: ScienceIDE 为智能体学习与科学实践的一体化工作空间奠定基础，使人类科学软件成为发展科学智能的共享基底。

Abstract: Scientific code repositories encode decades of human knowledge in executable models, methods, and tools. Yet fragmented toolchains, implicit domain conventions, and specialized correctness criteria make this knowledge difficult to convert into reliable learning experience-a challenge we call the scientific experience bottleneck. We introduce ScienceIDE, infrastructure for turning the world's scientific code into programmable environments for scientific agents. Guided by expert-defined scientific cases and acceptance criteria, agents transform repositories into executable environments that support task generation, execution, and scientific verification. These environments provide a shared foundation for supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and evaluation. Using verified interaction trajectories, we train PhAI-IDE-72B, PhAI-IDE-9B, and PhAI-IDE-4B. The model family shows gains in held-out scientific-code repair and across selected general-purpose benchmarks in code, reasoning, and knowledge, providing evidence of positive transfer from scientific experience to broader capabilities. ScienceIDE lays the foundation for an integrated workspace for agent learning and scientific practice, making humanity's scientific software a shared substrate for developing scientific intelligence. Code: https://github.com/aitofound/ScienceIDE

</details>


### [90] [A Zeroth-Order Paradigm for LLM Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19144)
*Peter Chen,Xi Chen,Wotao Yin,Tianyi Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析ComPO，一种基于比较oracle的零阶偏好对齐方法，通过从偏好对中提取方向信息而非直接优化可微偏好损失，缓解似然位移问题，并在多模型上超越现有直接对齐方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有直接偏好对齐方法虽计算与显存高效，但存在似然位移（likelihood displacement）问题，即在小似然间隔的偏好对上优化可微偏好损失时信息提取受限，因此需要探索从偏好对中提取信息的替代途径。

Method: 提出Comparison-based Preference Optimization（ComPO），一种基于比较oracle的零阶对齐方法，从偏好对中提取方向信息而不直接优化可微偏好损失。其基础离线方案在平滑性、梯度稀疏性及oracle与潜在目标兼容性的条件下建立收敛保证；进一步引入在线ComPO，保留离线比较机制并利用无标签策略生成进行相对参考策略的reverse-KL控制。基于覆盖视角，在局部覆盖与分布内成对奖励准确率下建立基本约束方案的性能保证。

Result: 在Mistral、Llama、Gemma-2、Qwen3与Gemma-3模型上的实验表明，ComPO优于现有直接对齐方法，包括长度控制胜率提升；成对级诊断结果为缓解似然位移提供了证据。

Conclusion: ComPO通过零阶比较oracle机制从偏好对中提取方向信息，提供了离线与在线方案的收敛与性能理论保证，并在多个LLM上验证了其有效性及对似然位移的缓解作用。

Abstract: Direct preference alignment methods are widely used to align large language models (LLMs) with human preferences because of their computational and memory efficiency. However, likelihood displacement motivates alternative ways to extract information from preference pairs with small likelihood margins. In this paper, we propose and analyze Comparison-based Preference Optimization (ComPO), a zeroth-order alignment method based on comparison oracles. ComPO extracts directional information from these pairs without directly optimizing a differentiable preference loss on them. We establish a convergence guarantee for its basic offline scheme under smoothness, gradient sparsity, and compatibility between the oracle and a latent objective. We further introduce online ComPO, which retains the offline comparison mechanism and uses unlabeled policy generations for reverse-KL control relative to a reference policy. Following the coverage perspective of preference fine-tuning, we establish a performance guarantee for a basic constrained scheme under local coverage and in-distribution pairwise reward accuracy. Experiments on Mistral, Llama, Gemma-2, Qwen3, and Gemma-3 models demonstrate improvements over existing direct alignment methods, including length-controlled win rates, with pair-level diagnostics providing evidence consistent with mitigating likelihood displacement.

</details>


### [91] [Objective vs. Search: Decomposing What Makes a Good Tokeniser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19145)
*Ahmetcan Yavuz,Clara Meister,Tiago Pimentel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将分词器设计解耦为优化目标（压缩 vs. 对数似然）与搜索过程（自底向上合并 vs. 自顶向下剪枝）两个正交维度，提出BottomUpLL与TopDownComp补齐2×2设计空间，发现搜索过程而非优化目标是影响语言建模性能的主导因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BPE与UnigramLM的比较同时混杂了优化目标（压缩 vs. 对数似然）与搜索过程（自底向上合并 vs. 自顶向下剪枝）两个维度，导致无法判断观测差异究竟源于"优化什么"还是"如何优化"。

Method: 引入两个新分词算法完成2×2设计空间：BottomUpLL（自底向上、基于似然）与TopDownComp（自顶向下、基于压缩）。用各算法产出的分词器训练语言模型，并变化模型规模、词表大小与领域（纯英语 vs. 多语言）。

Result: 在bits-per-byte指标上，搜索过程而非优化目标是主导因素：自底向上分词器在多数设置下稳定取得更低的bits-per-byte。但在BLiMP任务上，设计选择与性能之间未呈现一致关系。

Conclusion: 研究解耦了分词器设计选择对语言建模性能的影响，为更原则性的分词器构建提供了具体指导。

Abstract: Two dominant tokenisation algorithms are used by modern language models: byte-pair encoding (BPE) and UnigramLM. These differ along two orthogonal axes: their optimisation objective (compression vs. log-likelihood) and their search procedure (bottom-up merging vs. top-down pruning). Existing comparisons confound these axes, making it unclear whether their observed differences stem from what is being optimised vs. how it is being optimised. We disentangle the two by introducing two new tokenisation algorithms that complete this 2x2 design space: BottomUpLL, a bottom-up likelihood-based tokeniser, and TopDownComp, a top-down compression-based tokeniser. We train language models with tokenisers produced by each algorithm, varying: model size, vocabulary sizes, and domain (English-only vs. multilingual). Evaluating models on bits-per-byte, we find that the search procedure -- not the objective -- is the dominant factor: bottom-up tokenisers consistently achieve lower bits-per-byte in most settings. Evaluating models on the BLiMP task, however, shows no consistent relationship between design choice and performance. Overall, our results disentangle the effect of tokeniser design choices on language modelling performance, offering concrete guidance for their more principled construction.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [BLADE: ReliaBle Dynamic Hardware-Aware SNN-ANN Boundary SeLection for Event-BAseD Object DEtection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17562)
*Mahdi Taheri,Alwin Paul*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出BLADE，首个面向含ANN早退的动态混合SNN-ANN网络的可靠性感知边界选择方法，联合优化SNN-ANN边界与ANN早退配置，兼顾可靠性、精度、执行时间与能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合SNN-ANN网络采用静态推理，边界选择仅依据精度和能耗，未考虑动态推理与可靠性，难以满足安全攸关的边缘AI部署需求。

Method: BLADE通过设计空间探索中的层次化统计故障注入将可靠性纳入优化目标，联合优化SNN-ANN边界与ANN早退配置，面向可靠性、检测精度、执行时间和能耗进行多目标优化。

Result: 在事件型目标检测器上实现mAP 0.5为0.691，ANN早退触发时推理计算能耗降至15.82 mJ。可靠性分析表明，浮点指数位中最显著的位是灾难性失效的主因，其58.8%的故障注入导致显著或更严重精度退化；以约3%存储开销保护该位即可在评估的实际工艺故障率下消除灾难性失效。提高SNN计算占比可增强容错性，全SNN配置在激进故障条件下可靠性保持率达0.965。

Conclusion: 联合优化可靠性、精度、执行时间和能耗，可支持动态混合SNN-ANN系统在安全攸关边缘AI应用中更可靠的部署。

Abstract: Hybrid Spiking Neural Network (SNN)-Artificial Neural Network (ANN) architectures combine the energy efficiency of SNNs with the superior detection accuracy of ANNs for event-based object detection. Existing hybrid SNN--ANN networks, however, employ static inference and select the SNN-ANN boundary primarily according to accuracy and energy consumption, without considering dynamic inference or reliability. This paper presents BLADE, the first reliability-aware boundary selection methodology for dynamic hybrid SNN-ANN networks with ANN early exit. The proposed framework jointly optimizes the SNN-ANN boundary and ANN early-exit configuration according to reliability, detection accuracy, execution time, and energy consumption, while incorporating reliability through hierarchical statistical fault injection during design-space exploration. Experimental evaluation on an event-based object detector achieves an mAP 0.5 of 0.691 while reducing the inference compute energy to 15.82~mJ when the ANN early exit fires. Reliability analysis identifies the most significant floating-point exponent bit as the dominant source of catastrophic failures, producing significant-or-worse accuracy degradation in 58.8% of its fault injections. Protecting this single bit with approximately 3% storage overhead eliminates catastrophic failures across the evaluated realistic technology fault rates. Furthermore, increasing the proportion of SNN computation improves fault tolerance, with the fully SNN configuration achieving a reliability retention of 0.965 under aggressive fault conditions. The results demonstrate that jointly optimizing reliability, accuracy, execution time, and energy consumption enables more dependable deployment of dynamic hybrid SNN--ANN systems for safety-critical edge AI applications.

</details>


### [93] [Evolutionary Ensemble Search: Council-Guided Program Evolution with Persistent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17590)
*Juan P. Madrigal-Cianci,Eshan Chordia*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: EES通过专家引导的程序演化构建机器学习流程，采用角色分工的委员会生成搜索方向、编排器调度执行专家与演化引擎，并以验证证据门控集成与存档，在MLE-bench Lite上22个任务中19个达到奖牌阈值（86.36%），最佳为11金5银3铜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习流程构建多依赖人工专家试错，难以系统化累积跨任务与跨模态的可执行经验。本文旨在提出一种可累积、可执行、以证据驱动的自动化搜索架构，使程序演化过程中的候选解能够在计算结构变化时仍被公平比较。

Method: 核心机制包括四层：(1) 角色专业化委员会将任务证据与实验结果转化为结构化搜索方向；(2) 编排器把方向分配给执行专家和演化引擎；(3) 演化引擎选择已测量的父代、诊断其错误，并通过代码变异、结构化流水线编辑与交叉生成子代，每个子代必须实际执行并获得自身验证证据；(4) 种群存档保留有效备选方案，兼容的预测在验证门控的集成阶段竞争。搜索本身通过父代相对算子绩效、会话记忆以及跨运行检索的问题索引化经验进行自适应。文中还明确区分各机制的执行画像，并定义在候选计算发生变化时进行比较所需的契约。

Result: 在公开的MLE-bench Lite开发账本上，22个任务中有19个产生了达到奖牌阈值的成果，占86.36%；最佳结果为11枚金牌、5枚银牌和3枚铜牌。覆盖任务横跨文本、图像、表格、音频、科学几何以及确定性变换等多种模态。该活动还包含运行间的等级反馈、外部来源路径和混合确认流程。

Conclusion: 该工作报告了一个面向累积式可执行搜索的具体架构，并给出其跨模态开发成果的版本化记录。作者强调：账本中的总体成绩是已实现的开发结果，而非盲测自主智能体的成功率；其贡献在于可复用的架构设计、明确的比较契约以及可追溯的开发证据记录。

Abstract: Evolutionary Ensemble Search (EES) constructs machine-learning procedures through expert-guided program evolution. A role-specialized council turns task evidence and experimental results into structured search directions. An orchestrator allocates these directions to execution specialists and an evolutionary engine. The engine selects measured parents, diagnoses their errors, and produces descendants through code mutation, structured pipeline edits, and crossover. Each child must execute and acquire its own validation evidence. Population archives retain useful alternatives, while compatible predictions compete in a validation-gated ensemble stage. Search adapts through parent-relative operator credit, session memory, and problem-indexed lessons retrieved across runs. We specify these mechanisms, distinguish their execution profiles, and define the contracts required to compare candidates as their computations change. A public MLE-bench Lite development ledger records medal-threshold artifacts on 19 of 22 tasks (86.36\%), with best outcomes of 11 gold, five silver, and three bronze. The procedures span text, images, tables, audio, scientific geometry, and deterministic transformations. The campaign includes grade feedback between runs, external-source routes, and mixed confirmation procedures; its aggregate is an achieved development result, not a blind autonomous-agent success rate. The report contributes a concrete architecture for cumulative executable search and a versioned account of its cross-modal development outcomes.

</details>


### [94] [The Cost of Becoming: Developmental Encoding Increases Phenotypic Diversity but Reduces Locality and Recombination Robustness in Evolved Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17606)
*Lyes Saad Saoud*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 在严格配对的进化机器人基准中，发育编码并未在适应度上稳定超越直接编码，但显著提升表型多样性与发育期内损伤衰减，同时降低突变存活率、基因型-表型局部性与重组后代的适应度和存活率，揭示"生成性-局部性-稳健性-可继承性"的表示层权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发育编码常被寄望通过结构化基因型-表型映射提升可进化性、稳健性与适应性，但扩张表型变异可能同时削弱对已有父代结构的保真。作者希望检验这一被假设存在却缺乏受控验证的权衡。

Method: 采用受控进化机器人基准，比较三种相互匹配的表示：直接编码、静态生成式编码、时序合子发育编码。三者在基因组参数数（34）、成体控制器参数数（34）、初始基因组矩阵、优化器、突变与交叉算子、环境及适应度评估次数上完全一致；每种表示执行30次配对进化运行，并测量最终与分布外适应度、种群及突变可达表型多样性、突变存活率、基因型-表型局部性、交叉后代适应度/存活率/重组新颖性，以及发育早期与晚期损伤的对比。

Result: 发育编码在最终训练与分布外适应度上未能稳定超过直接编码，但显著提高最终种群表型多样性与突变可达表型多样性。同一表示表现出更低的突变存活率、更低的基因型-表型局部性、明显降低的交叉后代适应度与存活率，以及更高的重组新颖性。此外，持续的发育动力学使发育早期的损伤相对于条件相同的晚期损伤被衰减。

Conclusion: 结果识别出表示层面的权衡：发育可扩大可达表型邻域并提供发育期内损伤衰减，却损害已适应结构的局部继承。因此发育编码不应仅以性能评估，而应以生成性、局部性、稳健性与可继承性的联合几何来评价。

Abstract: Developmental encodings are often motivated by the expectation that a structured genotype-to-phenotype process can improve evolvability, robustness, and adaptation. Yet an encoding that expands phenotypic variation may simultaneously make useful parental structure harder to preserve under mutation and recombination. We test this trade-off in a controlled evolutionary-robotics benchmark comparing three matched representations: direct encoding, a static generative encoding, and a temporal zygotic developmental encoding. All three conditions use 34 genome parameters, 34 adult-controller parameters, identical initial genome matrices, the same optimizer, the same mutation and crossover operators, the same environments, and the same number of fitness evaluations. Across 30 paired evolutionary runs per representation, developmental encoding does not reliably exceed direct encoding in final training or out-of-distribution fitness. It does, however, substantially increase final population phenotypic diversity and mutant-reachable phenotype diversity. The same representation exhibits lower mutation viability, lower genotype--phenotype locality, sharply reduced crossover offspring fitness and viability, and increased recombination novelty. Continued developmental dynamics also attenuate damage applied early in development relative to otherwise identical late damage. These results identify a representation-level trade-off: development can enlarge the neighborhood of reachable phenotypes and provide within-development damage attenuation while degrading the local inheritance of already-adapted structure. We argue that developmental encodings should therefore be evaluated not by performance alone, but by a joint geometry of generativity, locality, robustness, and inheritance compatibility.

</details>


### [95] [Benchmarking Tabular Foundation Models as Surrogates in Expensive Evolutionary Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18130)
*Lu Han,Jin Wang,Yuchen Li,Haoran Gu,Shulei Liu,Ziyang Shi,Wenao Lu,Handing Wang*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文通过大量实验与理论分析，系统研究了TabPFN作为代理模型在代理辅助进化算法（SAEAs）中的有效性，发现其效果高度依赖问题类型，无法普遍替代传统代理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理模型在求解昂贵优化问题（EOPs）的SAEAs中起关键作用，而TabPFN凭借强大预测能力已被用作EOPs的代理模型并展现潜力，因此有必要系统评估其在SAEAs中的实际效果与适用边界。

Method: 在离线和在线SAEA两种设置下开展广泛实验，覆盖单目标、多目标、约束、组合、混合变量及工程优化等多类问题场景，并结合深入的理论分析，考察TabPFN的优势与局限。

Result: TabPFN的有效性高度依赖于具体问题，不能普遍替代传统代理模型；其表现受数据可用性、适应度景观复杂度、搜索空间特征及算法中角色等因素影响。

Conclusion: TabPFN应依据具体优化设置选择性采用，并需要定制化的模型管理策略与角色特定的算法设计，才能充分发挥其优势、规避其不足；文中还提供了实践指南。

Abstract: Surrogate-assisted evolutionary algorithms (SAEAs) are effective methods for solving expensive optimization problems (EOPs), where surrogate models replace most expensive evaluations and critically influence the final optimization results. In recent years, tabular foundation models have advanced rapidly, and the Tabular Prior-data Fitted Network (TabPFN) has been adopted as a surrogate model for EOPs due to its strong predictive capability, demonstrating promising performance. Motivated by its potential as a surrogate model in SAEAs, this work conducts a comprehensive study that combines extensive experiments with in-depth theoretical analysis to investigate the effectiveness of TabPFN. Specifically, we perform experiments across both offline and online SAEA settings, covering diverse problem scenarios such as single-objective, multi-objective, constrained, combinatorial, mixed-variable, and engineering optimization problems. In addition, we further analyze the advantages and limitations of TabPFN within SAEAs and provide practical guidelines for its application in different optimization settings. Results show that the effectiveness of TabPFN is highly problem dependent, and it cannot replace conventional surrogates universally. Overall, TabPFN should be adopted selectively according to data availability, landscape complexity, search space characteristics, and its role within the algorithm. Customized model management strategies and role-specific algorithm design are necessary to fully exploit its advantages and avoid its pitfalls.

</details>


### [96] [A Metaheuristic Optimization Framework for Discrete Optimization under Strict Time Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18702)
*Umut Çalıkyılmaz,Nitin Nayak,Sven Groppe*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出 STILO——一个面向严格时间限制的元启发式优化框架，集成 ACO、GA 与 SA 的细粒度配置空间及新算子，实验表明算法与算子的有效性依赖于问题类型、实例特征及可用计算预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时应用需要在毫秒级内求解困难问题并保证高质量解，元启发式优化框架（MOF）虽能提供通用搜索机制，但现有研究大多将可用计算时间视为隐含变量，未将其作为显式分析维度，导致难以针对严格时间预算选择合适算法与算子。

Method: 提出 STILO，一个专为严格时间限制下优化设计的元启发式优化框架。该框架为蚁群优化（ACO）、遗传算法（GA）和模拟退火（SA）集成了细粒度配置空间，融合已有算子与新型算子；其中包含面向 SA 的离散距离计算机制，以及面向 ACO 的与问题无关的图结构。在多种离散优化问题的合成实例与基准实例上开展实验。

Result: 实验表明：所提出的 SA 离散距离计算机制在严格时间限制下对优化有效；所提出的 ACO 问题无关图结构在不同时间限制下的相对有效性会变化，即使实例特征相同亦然。总体而言，算法族与算子的有效性不仅取决于问题类型，还取决于实例特征与可用计算预算。

Conclusion: 可用计算时间应作为优化分析的显式维度；在严格时间限制场景中，算法族与算子的选择需结合问题类型、实例特征及计算预算综合考量，STILO 为此类分析与实践提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Real-time applications often rely on optimization approaches that can find high-quality solutions to hard problems on the order of milliseconds. Metaheuristic optimization frameworks (MOFs) are useful tools for such tasks, as they provide large sets of general-purpose search mechanisms that can return solutions under different computational budgets. However, existing work largely overlooks the available computation time as an explicit dimension of analysis. In this work, we introduce STILO, a MOF specifically designed for optimization under strict time limits. STILO integrates fine-grained configuration spaces for ant colony optimization (ACO), genetic algorithm (GA), and simulated annealing (SA), combining existing and novel operators. We performed experiments using both synthetic and benchmark instances of various discrete optimization problems. The results indicate that the proposed discrete distance calculation mechanism for SA is useful for optimization under strict time limits. They also show that the relative effectiveness of the proposed problem-independent graph structures for ACO can vary across time limits, even for the same instance characteristics. More generally, the results demonstrate that the effectiveness of algorithm families and operators depends not only on the problem type, but also on the characteristics of the instance and the available computational budget.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [Pay Only for Disagreement: Certified No-Regression Verdicts for Model Updates with Matching Label-Complexity Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17560)
*Vishnu Bindu Balachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将模型更新上线（promotion）形式化为“配对风险差异审计”，提出基于无标签分歧输入的风险差恒等式，并构建两阶段序列协议 DISCERN：零标签层仅用无标签流量认证分歧率低于容差的良性更新，审计层仅对采样的分歧样本借助 anytime-valid 置信序列进行标注审计。理论上证明有限样本有效性与 ρ²/ε² 量级的标注复杂度界，说明利用无标签分歧可相对“配对盲”审计器节省 1/ρ 因子，且保证可在单一误差预算下跨无限次更新上线组合叠加。实验在 785 对更新、14,000+ 条重放审计流上验证：误覆盖 0.0002（名义 5%）、功效 0.986、零误报，56% 的良性更新以零标签认证，并输出机器可校验的证据记录用于上市后监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产模型会因重训练、微调、量化或供应商静默替换而不断更新，每次更新都可能比被替换版本更差，因此需要在“上线/推广”环节对更新是否劣化进行可靠审计。现有评估往往依赖全量标注或忽视两模型的配对结构，标注成本高且难以在持续更新场景下提供统计保证。

Method: 论文将更新推广形式化为“认证的配对风险差异审计”。关键起点是一个支持集恒等式：两模型的风险差只存在于二者预测不一致的输入上，且该不一致无需标签即可观测。基于此构建 DISCERN 两阶段序列协议：第一阶段为零标签层，仅利用无标签流量认证分歧率低于容差的良性更新；第二阶段为审计层，通过 anytime-valid 置信序列仅对采样的分歧样本进行标注，从而在任何停止时间和任何标签路由规则（甚至对抗性判定者）下保持有效性。理论上给出有限样本有效性与在速率层面 ρ²/ε² 的匹配标注复杂度界，并证明保证可在单一误差预算下跨无界更新序列组合。

Result: 在 785 对更新、14,000+ 条重放审计流（包括最高 1.4B 参数语言模型的 LoRA 微调）上评估：误覆盖率 0.0002（名义水平 5%），功效 0.986 且零误报，56% 的良性更新可在零标签条件下完成认证。每次审计还会生成机器可校验的证据记录，用于上市后监测。

Conclusion: DISCERN 为模型更新推广提供了一个兼具统计有效性、标注高效性与可组合性的审计框架：通过利用无标签分歧信息，证明相较配对盲审计器可节省 1/ρ 的标注复杂度，并能在持续更新中复用单一误差预算；实验表明其显著低于名义误覆盖、高功效、无误报，并能以零标签认证大量良性更新，同时提供可机器校验的审计证据。

Abstract: Every production model is updated, by retraining, fine-tuning, quantization, or a silent vendor swap, and each update risks being worse than what it replaced. We formalize update promotion as certified paired risk-difference auditing. Our starting point is a support identity: the risk difference between two models lives on the inputs where they disagree, observable without labels. We build DISCERN, a sequential two-tier protocol. A zero-label tier certifies benign updates whose disagreement rate is below tolerance from unlabeled traffic alone. An audited tier labels only sampled disagreements through an anytime-valid confidence sequence, valid at every stopping time and under any label-routing rule, even an adversarial judge. We prove finite-sample validity and matching label-complexity bounds of order rho^2/eps^2 at the rate level, so exploiting free disagreement provably saves a factor 1/rho over any pairing-blind auditor, and the guarantee composes across an unbounded sequence of promotions from one error budget. Across 14,000+ replayed audit streams over 785 update pairs, including LoRA fine-tunes of language models up to 1.4B parameters, miscoverage is 0.0002 (nominal 5%), power 0.986 with zero false alarms, and 56% of benign updates certify with zero labels. Each audit emits a machine-checkable evidence record for post-market monitoring.

</details>


### [98] [Beyond Static RAG: An Adaptive, Tri-Metric Routing Framework for Efficient Long-Context Inference on Commodity GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17564)
*Saipraveen Vabbilisetty,Ajay Kumar Boddepalli,Deep Narayan Mishra,Shashank Kapadia,Haoan Wang,Anupriya Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在16GB T4等消费级GPU上部署RAG时存在"压缩悖论"：神经提示压缩会带来KV缓存争用与预处理延迟，而不压缩则在长上下文下OOM。作者提出Tri-Metric Router，基于硬件物理信号（VRAM余量与延迟交叉点）在Raw、Neural（LLMLingua-2）和Lexical（BM25）三种流水线间进行免训练、确定性调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在内存预算紧张的商品GPU上共置部署vLLM服务的LLM与PyTorch压缩器时，会出现两类失败机制：神经压缩引入KV缓存争用和预处理延迟，其代价超过生成阶段节省的时间；而完全不做压缩又会在长上下文时触发显存溢出（OOM）。现有仅基于语义的自适应方法无法刻画这种由硬件物理约束主导的权衡，因此需要一种面向显存与延迟的调度策略。

Method: 提出Tri-Metric Router：一种确定性、免训练的策略，在Raw、Neural（LLMLingua-2）与Lexical（BM25）三种流水线之间选择。路由器使用三个CPU侧信号：空间复杂度L、句法密度ρ_key和类型-词元比（TTR）；与以往仅依赖语义的自适应不同，其调度信号基于硬件物理量——VRAM余量与延迟交叉点。阈值通过在LongBench qasper上的性能剖析进行校准，在T4上得到约4,332词的操作交叉点；论文的贡献在于该校准方法学而非某个硬件专属常数。

Result: 在分布外（OOD）留出集上，该方法实现0%的OOM失败率、88.5 ± 4.4%的oracle对齐度、49.3%的Combined F1，相比始终启用词法压缩提升5.2个百分点，且不增加额外显存开销或训练成本。

Conclusion: Tri-Metric Router通过硬件物理信号驱动的免训练调度，在紧张显存预算下有效规避了压缩悖论：既消除了长上下文OOM，又在精度上超过固定压缩策略，且不引入额外显存与训练成本。其核心价值在于可迁移的阈值校准方法学，而非针对特定硬件的固定常数。

Abstract: Deploying retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) on commodity GPUs such as the NVIDIA T4 (16 GB VRAM) exposes a practical failure mode we call the Compression Paradox: neural prompt compression can add key-value (KV) cache contention and preprocessing latency that outweigh generation-time savings, while skipping compression can cause out-of-memory (OOM) failures on long contexts. We identify two distinct failure mechanisms when a vLLM-served LLM and a PyTorch-based compressor are co-deployed under tight memory budgets, and introduce the Tri-Metric Router, a deterministic, training-free policy that selects among Raw, Neural (LLMLingua-2), and Lexical (BM25) pipelines. The router uses three CPU-side signals: spatial complexity ($L$), syntactic density ($ρ_{key}$), and type-token ratio (TTR). Unlike prior semantic-only adaptation, our dispatch signal is hardware-physical, based on VRAM headroom and a latency crossover point. Thresholds are calibrated from profiling on LongBench qasper, yielding an operating crossover near 4,332 words on T4; our contribution is this calibration methodology rather than a hardware-specific constant. On out-of-distribution holdouts, the method achieves 0% OOM failures, 88.5 $\pm$ 4.4% oracle alignment, and 49.3% Combined F1, improving over always-on lexical compression by 5.2 points without additional VRAM or training cost.

</details>


### [99] [Transformation Laws in Neural Representations: Structure, Realisability, and Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18190)
*Yuan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文以"变换律"为核心对象，研究神经表示中参考变换可通过兼容作用下降为可操作内容的条件，并以色相为例验证理论、测量结构与接口构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将表示分析与内部干预联系起来：理解神经表示如何保持输入变换的结构，并回答变换何时能在特征上被可操作地实现，从而把"理解表示"与"设计表示"统一到变换律这一具体对象上。

Method: 考察参考变换在神经特征上的兼容作用；刻画变换能"下降"通过编码器的条件，给出线性设定，其中缺陷由被丢弃信息在该表示所诱导度量下测得的"需求"控制。对整流器（rectifier）区分失败的两种来源：源区域已不可恢复的信息，以及用单一算子满足变换所经过的每个区域的代价；对"测量型"调和载体给出闭式判据——线性实现存在当且仅当保留的调和块在该作用下不变。以颜色为深度实例，测量冻结视觉特征中色相轨道的能量分布、旋转平面跨形状的共享性、其来源（输入与架构继承、训练与深度的重塑）以及测量结构对预测、迁移与复合的支持情况，并据此构造紧凑接口。

Result: 色相轨道在冻结视觉特征中84–88%能量集中于前两个谐波，旋转平面跨形状共享；该组织主要继承自输入与架构，并被训练与深度重塑；测量结构支持预测、从新起始状态迁移与复合，全局与局部实现在这些能力上差异显著。据此构造的接口其旋转作用由结构固定、不经拟合，在未见形状上零样本读取色相中位误差3.4°。

Conclusion: 理论、结构测量与构造三方面共同确立：变换律是连接神经表示理解与表示设计的可操作对象，且可据其构造免拟合、可迁移、可复合的紧凑接口。

Abstract: How neural representations preserve the structure of input changes connects representation analysis with internal intervention. We study operable representational content through compatible actions of reference transformations on neural features. We characterise when a transformation descends through an encoder, and give a linear setting in which the defect is governed by the transformation's demand for discarded information, measured in the metric the representation induces. On a rectifier the failure to realise a transformation has two distinguishable sources --- what the source region has already made unrecoverable, and what it costs to satisfy every region the transformation visits with one operator --- and for a \textit{measured} harmonic carrier the same question has a closed answer: a linear realisation exists exactly when the retained harmonic blocks are invariant under the action. Using colour as the in-depth instance, we find that hue orbits in frozen visual features concentrate 84--88\% of their energy in the first two harmonics with rotation planes shared across shapes, that this organisation is substantially inherited from input and architecture and is reshaped by training and depth, and that the measured structure supports prediction, transport from new starting states, and composition --- with global and local realisations differing sharply in which they achieve. Guided by the measurements, we construct a compact interface whose rotation action is fixed by the structure and never fitted: it reads hue zero-shot at 3.4$^\circ$ median error on unseen shapes. Theory, structural measurement, and construction together establish transformation laws as a concrete object connecting the understanding of neural representations to their design.

</details>


### [100] [Where Grokking Happens: Distributed Utility and Fourier Recoding Without a Module Switch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17571)
*Dekun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过"Transition Games"（行为对齐的精确激活博弈及配对非泛化对照）定位 Transformer 中从记忆到泛化转变的功能表达位置，发现该转变是既有分布式回路的谱重编码，而非模块切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究 Transformer 中"记忆到泛化"（grokking）转变在功能上究竟表达于何处，并检验"MLP 负责记忆、注意力负责泛化"这一流行预测是否成立。

Method: 提出 Transition Games：行为对齐的精确激活博弈，并设置成对的非泛化对照组；通过替换博弈（replacement games）量化 degree-two 模式的贡献，并用不相交的精确路径研究（exact path study）比较各下游路径的中介效应；同时检验路由起始、全局秩坍缩、素数不变架构脊等替代假设。

Result: 发现分布式效用增益，并伴随前瞻性的 block-0 注意力偏置；选定的 degree-two 模式在替换博弈中解释其加法对比的 67–92%；不相交精确路径研究显示 block-1 MLP 在 12/12 对中中介了比其他所有被测下游路径更多的效应。而"MLP 记忆、注意力泛化"的更强预测反而反转（记忆锚点处 −0.331 bits/example，0/12 符合预测方向）；路由起始、全局秩坍缩与素数不变架构脊均不成立。

Conclusion: 此处的 grokking 应被理解为既有分布式回路的谱重编码，而非某个模块的开关切换。

Abstract: Where in a Transformer is the change from memorization to generalization functionally expressed? We introduce Transition Games--behavior-aligned exact activation games with paired non-generalizing controls--and find distributed utility gain with a prospective block-0 attention bias; selected degree-two modes account for 67--92% of its addition contrast across replacement games, and a disjoint exact path study confirms that block-1 MLP mediates more of their effect than all other tested downstream paths in 12/12 pairs. The sharper "MLP memorizes, attention generalizes" prediction instead reverses (-.331 bits/example at the memory anchor; 0/12 in the predicted direction), while routing onset, global rank collapse, and a prime-invariant architecture ridge also fail, identifying grokking here as spectral recoding of an existing distributed circuit rather than a module switch.

</details>


### [101] [The evolution of sex for artificial intelligence: a population-genetic framework for multigenerational model populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18560)
*Giorgio F. Gilestro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将AI模型的多代演化过程类比为群体遗传学中的繁殖与遗传过程，建立跨学科形式化框架，并在递归训练、模型合并、大语言模型等场景中验证类比的成立及架构特异性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI开发中出现模型专门化、基于同伴输出再训练、权重平均合并等实践，导致模型形成"代际"。这些现象与群体遗传学研究的生物种群过程相似，但缺乏形式化的理论框架来理解多代模型群体的遗传规律。作者希望借助群体遗传学的成熟数学工具，为AI社会的"时间维度"演化提供可预测的理论基础。

Method: 作者将多代模型群体解释为有性与无性繁殖过程，形式化地融合两个领域。构建精确遗传模型，在训练网络（循环网络、前馈网络、变分自编码器生成器）和大语言模型上测试类比。将递归训练导致的模型崩溃对应为Wright-Fisher过程，真实数据作为移民项；分析父代输出平均对应混合遗传，最强贡献保留对应Fisher-Muller效应；并研究谱系生殖隔离现象。

Result: 类比普遍成立，但存在可测的架构特异性偏差。最小学习器模型精确复现Wright-Fisher过程；每代加入的真实数据起移民作用，且绝对样本数而非占比决定效果，与群体遗传学一致。对父代输出取平均会抵消多父代收益（混合遗传，重现Jenkin对达尔文的诘难），而保留各自最强贡献的合并方式可维持收益，合并的语言模型专家在各随机种子下均超越所有父代（Fisher-Muller效应）。当谱系学到冲突约定时发生生殖隔离、丧失合并能力，而单纯漂移分离则不会。

Conclusion: 随着AI社会在空间和时间维度上均成为社会，为其遗传建立数学框架具备预测能力。该框架几乎可以整体从生物学迁移适配而来，表明AI模型演化与生物种群遗传在数学结构上高度同构。

Abstract: Some aspects of AI development resemble a population process in which models are specialised, retrained on the output of peers, or combined by averaging weights. These practices lead to generations of models, in the biological sense studied by population genetics. Here, I develop this parallelism and interpret multigenerational model populations in terms of sexual and asexual reproduction, formally recombining the two fields. I test these analogies in an exact inheritance model, in trained networks (recurrent, feedforward and variational autoencoder generators) and in large language models, and show that they hold generally, with some measurable architecture-specific biases.
  Training recursively on model output is known to lead to model collapse, a process previously described as akin to genetic drift; I develop all that follows. A minimal model of a learner retrained on its parent's output reproduces the Wright-Fisher process exactly; verified real data added to each generation play the role of immigration, with the surprising finding that the absolute number of real data samples matters, not their share, exactly as in population genetics. Training a child on the average of its parents' outputs cancels the benefit of having several parents, matching blending inheritance (and reviving Jenkin's objection to Darwin), whereas combining parents so that each keeps its strongest contribution preserves it; merged language-model specialists exceeded every parent across seeds (the Fisher-Muller effect); and lineages become reproductively isolated, losing the ability to merge at all, when they have learned conflicting conventions and not when they have merely drifted apart.
  As AI societies become societies in time as well as in space, a mathematical framework for their inheritance acquires predictive power. Remarkably, that framework can be adapted almost wholesale from biology.

</details>


### [102] [Disentangling Algorithmic Bias from Archival Artifacts: A Controlled Audit of Vision-Language Model Valuation in Metropolitan Museum Archives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17572)
*Manpreet Singh,Rhythm Bhatia,Rahul Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以纽约大都会艺术博物馆开放获取的1,500件历史艺术品为样本，对CLIP类视觉-语言模型进行社会偏见审计，发现零样本评分的性别差异在统计上等价且不显著，但指出该指标本身接近嵌入噪声下限，仅属粗糙测量工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 审计视觉-语言模型的社会偏见时，需区分算法本身的估值差异与档案元数据中混杂因素（如媒介、年代、匿名作品）造成的伪相关，避免将机构收藏中的历史偏差误判为模型偏见。

Method: 构建量化审计框架：对OpenAI CLIP与OpenCLIP计算三类语义提示（杰作、质量、影响力）下的零样本logit差分评分；采用性别主效应检验、双单侧检验（TOST，Cohen's d ≥ 0.25）判定等价性；并以多元OLS回归控制艺术品媒介、创作年代与长宽比等混杂变量。

Result: 未调整评分高度收敛，性别主效应不显著（OpenAI CLIP：μ_F = -0.0067 vs μ_M = -0.0035, p = 0.1829；OpenCLIP：μ_F = 0.0171 vs μ_M = 0.0237, p = 0.1224），TOST确认等价（pTOST < 0.005）；多元回归显示性别条件效应不显著（p > 0.20），R² < 0.02，残差嵌入方差高，表明该指标接近嵌入噪声下限。

Conclusion: 宏观层面的评分等价反映的是指标对细粒度视觉-语义特征的不敏感，不能排除局部微观视觉偏见；同时排除41.2%未署名藏品会引入机构留存偏差。评估文化遗产AI公平性时，必须结合多元混杂控制、等价性检验与档案来源审计。

Abstract: Auditing vision-language models (VLMs) for societal bias requires distinguishing direct algorithmic valuation disparities from confounders embedded within archival metadata. In this study, we audit Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) models using historical artwork metadata from the Metropolitan Museum of Art Open Access collection (N = 1,500 total objects; N = 743 attributed works: Male n = 534, Female n = 209; n = 618 anonymous).
  We establish a quantitative audit framework evaluating zero-shot CLIP logit differential scores across three semantic prompt pairs (masterpiece, quality, and influence). Unadjusted evaluations demonstrate high score convergence without a statistically significant main gender effect under OpenAI CLIP (mu_F = -0.0067 vs mu_M = -0.0035, p = 0.1829) or OpenCLIP (mu_F = 0.0171 vs mu_M = 0.0237, p = 0.1224). Two One-Sided Tests (TOST) confirm statistical equivalence across Cohen's d >= 0.25 bounds (pTOST < 0.005).
  Multivariate OLS regression controlling for artwork medium, creation era, and aspect ratio (R^2 < 0.02) confirms that artist gender has no statistically significant conditional effect (p > 0.20). High residual embedding variance (R^2 < 2%) indicates that global zero-shot valuation metrics operate near an embedding noise floor, showing that broad zero-shot prompt logit differentials are a coarse measurement instrument rather than proving absolute model fairness.
  We highlight two key caveats: (i) macro-level score equivalence reflects metric insensitivity to fine-grained visual-semantic features and does not preclude localized micro-level visual biases, and (ii) excluding 41.2% unattributed holdings reflects institutional survival bias. These results demonstrate the necessity of multivariate confound control, equivalence testing, and archival provenance auditing when assessing AI fairness in cultural heritage collections.

</details>


### [103] [ReDIL-GNN: Resynthesis Domain Incremental Learning for Circuit Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18595)
*Rupesh Raj Karn,Johann Knechtel,Ozgur Sinanoglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReDIL-GNN框架，用逻辑重综合造成的域漂移研究电路GNN的域增量学习，并引入RAI指标在适配前评估新综合风格是否值得适配，从而实现部署控制闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑重综合在不改变任务标签的前提下改变门词汇、拓扑与结构统计量，给电路图神经网络带来域漂移。并非所有漂移都应盲目适配，因此需要一种在适配前判断新综合风格是否可支持、是否值得更新的机制。

Method: 提出ReDIL-GNN重综合域增量学习框架：在新综合风格到来时适配固定的预测头或表示头，并在所有已观测域上评估保持性。进一步提出重综合可适配性指数RAI，作为适配前评分，综合适配需求、源等价可恢复性、结构覆盖率与更新兼容性。评估涵盖监督式硬件安全任务与表示学习模型，分类器用任务原生指标，嵌入模型用源等价检索指标，并与朴素微调、LwF、Online EWC、MAS、ER、A-GEM、DER++、ER+LwF及等价引导回放对比。

Result: 在所研究的流程中，RAI能区分不支持性漂移与有潜力的更新，得分从结构未覆盖的GNN-RE ABC重写漂移的0.001，到最佳仅在原始域适配的GNN-RE场景的0.824。

Conclusion: ReDIL-GNN将重综合感知的电路学习转化为部署控制闭环：RAI在更新前筛查每个新综合流程，指导复用当前模型、执行保持性感知适配，或推迟适配直至该漂移获得更好支持。

Abstract: Logic resynthesis preserves circuit functionality while changing gate vocabulary, topology, and structural statistics, creating domain shift for circuit graph neural networks (GNNs) without changing task labels. To study this setting, we introduce ReDIL-GNN, a resynthesis domain-incremental learning framework that adapts a fixed prediction or representation head as new synthesis styles arrive and evaluates retention on all previously observed domains. Because not every shift should be adapted blindly, ReDIL-GNN further introduces the Resynthesis Adaptability Index (RAI), a pre-adaptation score that combines adaptation need, source-equivalence recoverability, structural coverage, and update compatibility. We evaluate supervised hardware-security tasks and representation-learning models using task-native metrics for classifiers and source-equivalence retrieval metrics for embedding models, comparing naive fine-tuning with LwF, Online EWC, MAS, ER, A-GEM, DER++, ER+LwF, and equivalence-guided replay. Across the studied pipelines, RAI separates unsupported shifts from promising updates, ranging from 0.001 for a structurally uncovered GNN-RE ABC-rewrite shift to 0.824 for the best original-only GNN-RE adaptation case. In practice, ReDIL-GNN turns resynthesis-aware circuit learning into a deployment control loop: RAI screens each new synthesis flow before update, guiding whether to reuse the current model, apply retention-aware adaptation, or defer adaptation until the shift is better supported.

</details>
