Hence every finite simple graph, and more generally every hypergraph independence system without singleton forbidden hyperedges, is representable by an acyclic single-source gadget. We then specialise the construction to odd cycles. For C_{2k+1}, a uniform rational family produces a fractional cheap-selection vector that violates the odd-cycle inequality. A potential shift converts a signed connector separator into nonnegative arc costs and gives the exact cost-preserving additive-congestion threshold tau = 1 - bq. Within the symmetric family, the supremum threshold is (k+2)/(2(k+1)), which tends to 1/2. For C5, an exact certificate independently derives all source-terminal paths and enumerates all 3^10 = 59049 unsplittable routings using rational arithmetic.

</details>


### [206] [Low-Degree Polynomial Approximation of the Cross-Polytope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17614)
*Xiaoyu Li*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文确定了 $d$ 维交叉多面体 $B_1^d$ 多项式逼近的"次数-失真"权衡：非负形式与 SoS 形式的最优失真均为 $Θ(\sqrt{d/t})$，常数失真当且仅当次数为 $Θ(d)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画非负多项式形式与 sum-of-squares (SoS) 形式在逼近 $d$ 维交叉多面体 $B_1^d$ 时的最优性，明确给定次数 $2t$ 下可达到的最小乘性 sandwich 失真，并厘清非负形式下界与 SoS 上界之间的紧致关系。

Method: 下界（表示无关）：对符号置换取平均并在 $\ell_1$ 球面 flat points 上求值，将任意候选形式化为支撑大小剖面 $V(k)=k^{-2t}Q(k)$（$°Q\le t$, $Q(0)=0$），经 $u=1/k$ 替换与 Lagrange 插值证明该次数预算无法在 $d$ 个支撑尺度上保持剖面近似恒定。上界：构造平均符号向量 facet 法向偶次幂的显式 SoS 形式，归约为 Rademacher 矩估计。另给出加权倒数网格引理、$\ell_p$ 球推论及与极坐标立方体的对比；对一般对称多面体，用加权 facet 幂构造单侧证书，边界地板目标函数凹，最差方向 oracle 归约为凸对偶范数问题，次数为二时最优解回到经典最优设计与 John 椭球。

Result: 对每个 $1\le t\le d$，任何远离原点为正的全局非负 $2t$ 次形式，其乘性 sandwich 失真至少为 $(2e)^{-1/2}\sqrt{d/t}$；而显式 SoS 形式达到至多 $(2e)^{1/2}\sqrt{d/t}$。故非负形式与 SoS 最优值均为 $Θ(\sqrt{d/t})$，且常数失真需且仅需次数 $Θ(d)$。一般对称多面体的单侧证书优化中，次数二最优解可恢复经典最优设计与 John 椭球。

Conclusion: 该结果为交叉多面体建立了紧致的次数-失真权衡，下界不依赖具体表示，上界由显式 SoS 构造匹配，二者同阶。所提出的证书优化属于 oracle 模型框架，并非端到端复杂度结果，也未完全刻画整个 SoS 最优解。

Abstract: We determine the degree-distortion tradeoff for polynomial approximation of the $d$-dimensional cross-polytope $B_1^d$. For every $1\le t\le d$, every globally nonnegative degree-$2t$ form that is positive away from the origin has multiplicative sandwich distortion at least $(2e)^{-1/2}\sqrt{d/t}$, while an explicit sum-of-squares (SoS) form achieves distortion at most $(2e)^{1/2}\sqrt{d/t}$. Hence both the nonnegative-form and SoS optima are $Θ(\sqrt{d/t})$, and degree $Θ(d)$ is necessary and sufficient for constant distortion. The lower bound is representation-free. Averaging over signed permutations and evaluating on flat points of the $\ell_1$ sphere reduces every candidate to a support-size profile $V(k)=k^{-2t}Q(k)$ with $°Q\le t$ and $Q(0)=0$. After the substitution $u=1/k$, Lagrange interpolation shows that this degree budget cannot keep the profile nearly constant across $d$ support scales. A matching SoS construction averages even powers of sign-vector facet normals and reduces the upper bound to a Rademacher moment. We also isolate a weighted reciprocal-grid lemma, derive consequences for $\ell_p$ balls, and contrast the polar cube. For a general symmetric polytope, weighted facet powers yield a one-sided certificate whose boundary-floor objective is concave and whose worst-direction oracle reduces to convex dual-norm problems; at degree two, its optimizers recover classical optimal design and the John ellipsoid. This is an oracle-model certificate optimization, not an end-to-end complexity result or a characterization of the full SoS optimum.

</details>


### [207] [Intrinsic-Dimensional Wasserstein Guarantees for Private Synthetic Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17624)
*Yiyun He*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文利用 PrivTree 自适应二叉划分并对叶质量做差分隐私释放，构造 [0,1]^d 上 n 点的 ε-差分隐私合成测度，在最坏情形数据模型下给出最优的 1-Wasserstein 误差率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究差分隐私合成测度在无采样或总体分布假设的最坏情形数据模型下的误差率，目标是使误差依赖数据集的内在维度而非环境维度 d。

Method: 对 [0,1]^d 中 n 点应用 PrivTree 构造自适应二叉划分，并对其叶节点质量进行隐私释放；进一步引入 shifting 技术降低常数对环境维度 d 的指数依赖。

Result: d≥2 时 1-Wasserstein 误差为 \widetilde O_d((εn)^{-1/d})，与极小极大下界相比最优（至多对数因子）；当 d≥3、2<s≤d 且数据集在相关有限尺度 r 上覆盖数至多为 Ar^{-s} 时，期望误差改进为 \widetilde O_{d,s}((εn)^{-1/s})。

Conclusion: 误差率取决于有限尺度内在维度而非环境维度，且无需恢复低维流形；shifting 技术可进一步避免常数对环境维度 d 的指数依赖。

Abstract: We study an $\varepsilon$-differentially private synthetic measure for $n$ points in $[0,1]^d$ by applying the existing PrivTree algorithm to construct an adaptive binary partition and then privately releasing its leaf masses. We consider the worst-case data model without any sampling or population-distribution assumption. The 1-Wasserstein error of the synthetic measure is $\widetilde O_d((\varepsilon n)^{-1/d})$ for $d\ge2$, which is optimal compared to the minimax lower bound up to a logarithmic factor.
  Moreover, for $d\ge3$ and $2<s\le d$, if the data set has covering number at most $Ar^{-s}$ over the relevant finite range of scales $r$, the expected error improves to $\widetilde O_{d,s}((\varepsilon n)^{-1/s})$. Thus the rate depends on a finite-scale intrinsic dimension rather than the ambient dimension, without requiring the recovery of a low-dimensional manifold. We also introduce a shifting technique to further avoid the exponential dependence of the constant on the ambient dimension $d$.

</details>


### [208] [Tight Lower Bounds for Differentially Private Continual Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17650)
*Charlie Harrison,Ethan Leeman*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文解决了二叉机制（Binary Tree Mechanism）在纯差分隐私下持续计数的最优性问题，证明其渐近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二叉机制是差分隐私持续计数的标准算法，但自提出以来，其在纯差分隐私下的渐近最优性一直未解决。本文旨在解决这一悬而未决的问题。

Method: 证明基于一个单一困难分布，该分布在树上具有有界指数得分（bounded exponential score）。通过对得分进行简单修改，同一框架可适用于三种误差度量。

Result: 对于固定0<ε≤1，证明了最坏情况期望ℓ∞误差的Ω(log²n)下界，以及每坐标均值与最大期望平方误差的Ω(log³n)下界。这些下界适用于任意机制，即使整个流事先已知。当δ≤n^{-c}（任意固定c>0）时，近似差分隐私下同样成立。下界与使用拉普拉斯噪声的二叉机制相匹配。

Conclusion: 本文建立了二叉机制在纯差分隐私及近似差分隐私（δ≪1/n的标准情形）下的渐近最优性。

Abstract: The Binary Tree Mechanism is a standard algorithm for differentially private continual counting, but its asymptotic optimality under pure differential privacy has remained unresolved since its introduction. We resolve this question. For fixed $0 < \varepsilon \le 1$, we prove asymptotically tight lower bounds of $Ω(\log^2 n)$ for worst-case expected $\ell_\infty$ error and $Ω(\log^3 n)$ for mean and maximum per-coordinate expected squared error. These bounds hold for arbitrary mechanisms, even when the entire stream is available in advance. The same lower bounds hold under approximate differential privacy whenever $δ\le n^{-c}$, for any fixed $c>0$. Our lower bounds match the Binary Tree Mechanism instantiated with Laplace noise, establishing its asymptotic optimality under both pure differential privacy and approximate differential privacy in the standard regime of $δ\ll1/n$. Our proof uses a single hard distribution with a bounded exponential score on a tree. A simple modification of the score allows the same framework to establish tight lower bounds for all three error measures.

</details>


### [209] [Efficient Robust Learning at the Information-Theoretic Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17655)
*Adam R. Klivans,Konstantinos Stavropoulos,Sergei Tikhonov,Arsen Vasilyan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文解决了Blanc(2026)遗留的开放问题：在给定ERM预言机访问下，给出多项式时间算法，实现对固定分布上布尔概念类的鲁棒学习，达到最优误差 $\eta+\varepsilon$；关键创新在于使用无遗憾学习器，并额外给出了基于超收缩分布下夹逼多项式的无需ERM预言机的高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Blanc(2026)虽给出了达到最优误差 $\eta+\varepsilon$ 的鲁棒学习算法（区别于确定性假设不可能低于 $2\eta+\varepsilon$ 的局限），但其算法计算效率不足。核心动机是寻找在ERM预言机访问下的多项式时间算法，以弥补效率缺口。同时，希望弱化对ERM预言机的依赖。

Method: 1. 利用多种类型的无遗憾学习器（no-regret learners）设计多项式时间算法，在访问ERM预言机条件下求解鲁棒学习问题。2. 针对允许在超收缩分布上实现夹逼多项式（sandwiching polynomials）的函数类，给出无需ERM预言机的高效算法。

Result: 1. 在ERM预言机访问下，对固定分布的布尔概念类给出了达到最优误差 $\eta+\varepsilon$ 的多项式时间鲁棒学习算法，解决了Blanc的开放问题。2. 对允许超收缩分布下夹逼多项式的函数类，给出了无需ERM预言机的高效算法。3. 作为推论，首次给出针对高斯边缘分布下学习半空间的多项式时间算法，达到任意常数 $\varepsilon$ 下的误差 $\eta+\varepsilon$。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入无遗憾学习器技术，成功给出了Blanc遗留问题的多项式时间解，并进一步扩展到基于夹逼多项式和超收缩分布的高效鲁棒学习框架，实现了高斯边缘下半空间鲁棒学习的首个多项式时间算法。

Abstract: In an important recent work, Blanc (2026) gave an algorithm for robustly learning Boolean concept classes with respect to a fixed distribution that outputs a (randomized) classifier achieving the optimal error of $η+ \varepsilon$ where $η$ is the noise rate. In contrast, it is well known that deterministic hypotheses cannot achieve error less than $2η+ \varepsilon.$
  Blanc's algorithm is computationally inefficient, and the main problem left open in his work is to find a polynomial-time algorithm given access to an oracle for empirical risk minimization (ERM). In this paper, we resolve this problem and give such an algorithm. Perhaps surprisingly, our techniques make crucial use of various types of no-regret learners.
  Additionally, we give an efficient algorithm (no ERM oracle required) for robustly learning any function class that admits sandwiching polynomials with respect to hypercontractive distributions. As one consequence, we give the first polynomial-time algorithm for robustly learning a halfspace with respect to Gaussian marginals that achieves error $η+ \varepsilon$ for any constant $\varepsilon$.

</details>


### [210] [A tight 1/3-approximation algorithm and fully polynomial-time approximation schemes for the Colored Knapsack Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17713)
*Fabio Ciccarelli,Fabio Furini*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了有色背包问题（ColKP）的近似算法，提出了一个最坏情况性能比为1/3的线性时间近似算法，并基于利润标度技术构造了两个全多项式时间近似方案（FPTAS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ColKP将经典背包问题推广为带颜色约束的形式，要求所选物品存在一个相邻颜色各不相同的排序，该问题弱NP难，文献中仅有运行时间为$O(b n^4)$和$O(b^2 n^3)$的两种伪多项式DP算法，但此前不存在任何近似算法，因此有必要研究其近似算法与FPTAS。

Method: 首先，通过对自然整数规划形式线性规划松弛的最优基解进行取整并修复颜色可行性，给出线性时间近似算法；其次，将两种DP算法重新表述为以利润（而非背包容量）为伪多项式维度的形式，并结合利润标度技术分别构造两个FPTAS。

Result: 所提线性时间近似算法的最坏情况性能比为1/3；第一个FPTAS在非负利润下运行时间为$O(n^5/\varepsilon)$，在任意整数利润下为$O(n^6/\varepsilon)$；第二个FPTAS对应运行时间分别为$O(n^5/\varepsilon^2)$和$O(n^7/\varepsilon^2)$。

Conclusion: 该近似保证及新的结构性洞察为控制标度后的利润范围提供了所需界，从而确立了上述FPTAS的运行时间，填补了ColKP近似算法研究的空白。

Abstract: The $\textit{Colored Knapsack Problem}$ (ColKP) generalizes the classical Knapsack Problem by partitioning the items into color classes and requiring the selected items to admit an ordering in which consecutive items have different colors. The problem is weakly $\mathcal{NP}$-hard and admits two pseudo-polynomial dynamic programming (DP) algorithms proposed in the literature. These two DP algorithms have worst-case running times $O(b \, n^4)$ and $O(b^2 \, n^3)$, respectively, where $b$ is the knapsack capacity and $n$ is the number of items. We develop the first approximation algorithm for the ColKP. By rounding an optimal basic solution of the linear programming relaxation of its natural integer programming formulation and repairing color feasibility, we obtain a linear-time approximation-algorithm whose worst-case performance ratio is $1/3$. We then reformulate both DP algorithms so that profit, rather than knapsack capacity, indexes their pseudo-polynomial dimension, and combine them with profit scaling to obtain two fully polynomial-time approximation schemes (FPTASs). The first FPTAS runs in $O(n^5/\varepsilon)$ time for nonnegative profits and in $O(n^6/\varepsilon)$ time for arbitrary integer profits. The second FPTAS runs instead in $O(n^5/\varepsilon^2)$ and $O(n^7/\varepsilon^2)$ time, respectively. The approximation guarantee, along with new structural insights, provides the bounds needed to control the scaled profit range and establish these running times.

</details>


### [211] [A 3.7321-Competitive Algorithm for Matroid Secretary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17782)
*Hau Chan,Jianan Lin,Chenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文在仅使用元素数量与已到达元素独立性查询的信息模型下，将任意拟阵的拟阵秘书问题的竞争比从4改进到2+√3≈3.7321，查询复杂度为O(n²)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟阵秘书问题要求在线算法在均匀随机到达、即时且不可撤销决策下选出高权重独立集。Singla(2026)在受限信息模型下给出4-竞争算法，本文旨在同一模型下改进竞争比。

Method: 沿用Singla的可逆参考过程，但保留随机抽取的一部分样本作为"储备"，其是否属于参考贪心解不被冻结；通过平衡剩余样本与样本后元素维持可逆性，从而精确计算交换伙伴阻塞目标元素的概率。

Result: 竞争比改进为2+√3≈3.7321；算法以至少2−√3的概率接受某个固定规范最优解的每个元素；使用O(n²)次独立性查询，并给出直接解析证明。

Conclusion: 该工作在同一信息模型下严格优于Singla的4-竞争结果，提供了具解析证明的可逆性保留方法。

Abstract: The matroid secretary problem asks an online algorithm to select a high-weight independent set from elements arriving in uniformly random order, with immediate and irrevocable decisions. Singla (2026) recently gave a $4$-competitive algorithm for arbitrary matroids using only the number of elements and independence queries on already-arrived elements. Following his approach, we obtain an improved competitive ratio of $2+\sqrt3\approx3.7321$ in the same information model. Our algorithm accepts every element of a fixed canonical optimum with probability at least $2-\sqrt3$ and uses $O(n^2)$ independence queries. The algorithm modifies Singla's reversible reference process by retaining a randomly chosen part of the sample as a reserve whose membership in the reference greedy solution is not frozen. Balancing the remaining sample and post-sample elements preserves reversibility and allows an exact calculation of the probability that an exchange partner blocks a target element. The resulting guarantee has a direct analytic proof.

</details>


### [212] [A Better-Than-$3$ Approximation Algorithm for Demand Matching via Knapsack Intersection LP and Contention Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17932)
*Michel X. Goemans,Yuchong Pan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文研究需求匹配问题（推广了背包问题与b-匹配问题），首次给出优于3的近似比：一般图随机(3/2+√2+ε)≈2.914+ε近似算法，二部图随机(2+ε)近似算法。方法基于相交整数背包多面体加强的LP松弛，并证明存在(q,1/(1+q))-平衡的争用消解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求匹配问题中，若每条边单独可行，Parekh证明自然LP松弛的整性间隙至多3，得到3-近似算法，且该界对自然LP是紧的（Shepherd与Vetta的下界）。论文旨在打破3这一屏障，获得严格优于3的近似比。

Method: 采用基于在顶点处相交整数背包多面体的加强LP松弛，并结合多选（multiple-choice）推广；关键工具是为整数背包多面体构造(q,1/(1+q))-平衡的争用消解方案，对任意q∈[0,1]成立。算法运行时间为1/ε与输入长度的多项式。

Result: 一般图得到随机(3/2+√2+ε)≈2.914+ε近似算法；二部图得到随机(2+ε)近似算法，均为首次优于3的结果。同时证明背包多面体的平衡保证1/(1+q)在最坏情况下是紧的。

Conclusion: 论文通过加强LP松弛与平衡争用消解方案，将需求匹配的近似比从紧界3改进到约2.914（一般图）和2（二部图），且所用平衡争用消解方案可独立应用于其他问题。

Abstract: The demand matching problem generalizes both the knapsack problem and the $b$-matching problem. In this problem, each edge of a graph has a demand and a weight, and each vertex has a capacity. The goal is to find a maximum weight subset of edges such that, at each vertex, the total demand of the incident selected edges does not exceed the vertex capacity. Parekh [IPCO 2011] proved that, if each edge is individually feasible, the natural LP relaxation for demand matching has integrality gap at most $3$, yielding a $3$-approximation algorithm. This bound is tight for the natural LP relaxation, matching the lower bound of Shepherd and Vetta [Math. Oper. Res. 2007].
  We present a randomized $(3/2 + \sqrt{2} + \varepsilon) \approx (2.914 + \varepsilon)$-approximation algorithm for the demand matching problem for every $\varepsilon > 0$, giving the first approximation ratio strictly better than $3$. For bipartite graphs, we obtain a randomized $(2 + \varepsilon)$-approximation algorithm for every $\varepsilon > 0$. Both algorithms run in time polynomial in $1/\varepsilon$ and the input length. Our algorithms use a strengthened LP relaxation based on intersecting the integral knapsack polytopes associated with the vertices, together with a multiple-choice generalization. As a key ingredient, we prove the existence of a $(q, 1/(1+q))$-balanced contention resolution scheme for the integral knapsack polytope for every $q \in [0, 1]$, which may be of independent interest. The balance guarantee $1/(1+q)$ is tight in the worst case over all knapsack instances.

</details>


### [213] [Systematic Data Structure Lower Bounds via the Query-with-Sketch Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18024)
*Sumegha Garg,Songhua He,Yuanzhi Li,Periklis A. Papakonstantinou,Xin Yang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究近似矩阵幂（AMP）问题的数据结构下界，提出"查询-草图"模型与基于最小熵的通用框架，在系统化（存储原始矩阵+冗余比特）体制下证明探测次数与冗余比特之间的权衡，并以此支持Patrascu–Roditty (2010)关于常数时间集合不相交查询空间复杂度的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似矩阵幂是图算法与谱方法中的基础原语，但其在系统化（数据逐字存储）体制下的探测-冗余权衡缺乏下界证据；同时Patrascu–Roditty关于常数时间集合不相交查询所需空间的猜想长期缺少无条件证据。作者希望建立通用框架以推导此类权衡。

Method: 提出"查询-草图"（query-with-sketch）模型作为证明系统化数据结构探测-冗余权衡的通用框架；发展基于最小熵（min-entropy）的方法，将无冗余情形下的条件最小熵下界提升为有冗余情形下的探测下界；随后针对AMP及其变体，运用问题相关的解析与代数工具建立所需的最小熵界。

Result: 在系统化体制下（数据存储M的逐字内容、额外r比特冗余、少量探测），对近似矩阵幂问题获得了探测次数与冗余比特之间的权衡下界；该结果同时为常数时间集合不相交查询的空间需求提供了新的无条件证据，支持Patrascu–Roditty (2010)的猜想。

Conclusion: 论文确立了系统化数据结构探测-冗余权衡的一般性证明框架，并通过AMP及其变体展示了其适用性；所得下界为Patrascu–Roditty猜想提供了新的无条件支持，表明在低冗余下实现高效查询需要非平凡的探测开销。

Abstract: We study data structure lower bounds for the Approximate Matrix Powering (AMP) problem. Given a substochastic, symmetric matrix $\mathbf{M}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times n}$ and parameters $k$ and $α$, the goal is to preprocess $\mathbf{M}$ so as to answer entry queries $(u,v)\mapsto \mathbf{M}^{k}[u,v]$ up to additive error $1/n^α$. We focus on AMP in the succinct and systematic regime, in which the data structure stores $\mathbf{M}$ verbatim, uses an additional $r$ bits of redundancy, and must answer queries by probing only a small number of entries of $\mathbf{M}$.
  Our main conceptual contribution is a general framework for proving probe--redundancy trade-offs for systematic data structures. We introduce the query-with-sketch model and develop a min-entropy-based approach that lifts conditional min-entropy bounds in the absence of redundancy to probe lower bounds in the presence of redundancy. We then establish these min-entropy bounds using problem-specific analytic and algebraic tools, for the downstream applications to AMP and its variants. As a consequence, our results provide new unconditional evidence toward a conjecture of Patrascu and Roditty (2010) on the space required for constant-time set-disjointness queries.

</details>


### [214] [Serial-batch scheduling to minimise the total weighted late work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18046)
*Yao-Wen Sang,Naiming Xie,Jian Chen,Malgorzata Sterna,Jacek Blazewicz*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究串行批处理机上最小化总加权迟工量问题，证明其NP-困难并给出伪多项式时间动态规划算法及加速技术，实验表明算法在时间效率上优于Gurobi。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 串行批处理调度具有实际应用背景，而总加权迟工量是衡量交货性能的重要指标。该问题此前缺乏系统的复杂性分析与高效求解方法，因此需要明确其计算复杂度并提供有效算法。

Method: 针对串行批处理机（批内作业顺序加工、批加工时间为批内作业时间之和、批内作业完成时间等于该批完工时间、每批需常数准备时间）建模；证明即使在共同工期和单位权重下问题仍为NP-困难；对一般问题设计伪多项式时间动态规划算法；针对共同工期和工期-加工时间-权重一致（agreeable）两类特殊情况分别设计专门伪多项式时间动态规划算法，并配备专门加速技术提升计算性能。

Result: 证明了目标问题在共同工期、单位权重情形下仍为NP-困难；给出了一般问题的伪多项式时间动态规划算法；针对两类特殊情况分别给出专门的伪多项式时间动态规划算法；扩展实验显示所提动态规划算法在时间效率上优于Gurobi。

Conclusion: 该研究界定了串行批处理机总加权迟工量调度问题的计算复杂度，并提供通用与专用动态规划求解框架及加速策略，验证了其相对商业求解器的效率优势。

Abstract: We study the problem of scheduling jobs on a serial-batch machine with the aim of minimising the total weighted late work. In a serial-batch setting, jobs within a batch are processed sequentially, and none are removed from the machine until the last job in the batch completes its processing. The processing time of a batch is the sum of the processing times of the jobs within it, and the completion time for each job in the batch is equal to the makespan of the jobs in the batch. When a new batch begins, a constant setup time is required for the machine. We show that minimising the total weighted late work in this environment is $NP$-hard even if all jobs have a common due date and unit weight. For the general problem, we present a pseudo-polynomial time dynamic programming algorithm. Additionally, we explore two special cases, i.e., one with a common due date and another with an agreeable condition among due dates, processing times and weights. For both special cases, we develop specialised pseudo-polynomial time dynamic programming algorithms. The proposed approaches are equipped with specialised acceleration techniques to enhance their computational performance. The extended experiments demonstrate that the dynamic programming algorithms outperform Gurobi in time efficiency.

</details>


### [215] [Efficient Algorithms for Subdeterminant Maximization under Partition Matroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18103)
*Nikhil Bansal,Yuze Xu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究划分拟阵约束下的行列式最大化问题，给出 $e^{O(k)}$ 近似算法，改进了此前 $k^{O(k)}$ 的近似比，并达到与最佳估计保证（$e^k$，至多相差指数上的 $O(1)$ 因子）相匹配的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行列式最大化在划分拟阵约束下具有重要理论与应用价值。此前最优近似比为 $k^{O(k)}$（$k$ 为拟阵秩），而相应的估计（estimation）保证为 $e^k$，二者之间存在显著差距。本文旨在缩小这一近似与估计之间的差距。

Method: 基于 Nikolov-Singh（2016）提出的几何 max-min 松弛进行舍入；采用连续的势函数驱动过程（continuous potential-driven process）；并利用该松弛的若干新的结构与解析性质。

Result: 获得 $e^{O(k)}$ 近似算法，用于在划分拟阵的基中寻找使 $\det(A_{S,S})$ 最大化的集合 $S$，其中 $k$ 为 $M$ 的秩。

Conclusion: 该算法将近似比从 $k^{O(k)}$ 改进至 $e^{O(k)}$，在指数层面与 $e^k$ 的估计保证仅相差 $O(1)$ 因子，从而基本弥合了近似与估计之间的差距。

Abstract: We consider the determinant maximization problem under partition constraints: Given an $n\times n$ PSD matrix A and a partition matroid $M$ on $[n]$, find a base $S$ of $M$ that maximizes $\det(A_{S,S})$. We give an $e^{O(k)}$-approximation algorithm to find such a set $S$, where $k$ is the rank of $M$. This improves upon the current $k^{O(k)}$-approximation, and matches the current $e^k$-estimation guarantee, up to $O(1)$ factors in the exponent. Our algorithm is based on rounding the geometric max-min relaxation due to Nikolov-Singh'2016, using a continuous potential-driven process, and several new structural and analytic properties of this relaxation.

</details>


### [216] [Learning Depth-3 Circuits with Polynomial Savings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18166)
*Xi Chen,Animesh Fatehpuria,Shyamal Patel,Rocco Servedio*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文在mistake-bound在线学习模型下学习深度三电路，对任意常数γ≥1，学习规模n^γ的深度三电路，运行时间从之前的2^{n-Ω(n/log n)}改进为2^{n-c_γ n}，其中c_γ仅依赖于γ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度三电路在mistake-bound在线学习模型下的学习是一个困难问题，该模型比无分布PAC学习更难。先前Servedio和Tan [ST17]的算法只能以2^{n-Ω(n/log n)}的时间学习poly(n)规模深度三电路，即相对朴素记忆化方法仅节省N^{o(1)}倍，改进空间有限。

Method: 核心是改进宽度-k CNF的近似度界。受Szegedy [Sze04]和Magniez等 [MNRS11]启发，利用Chebyshev多项式高效放大精心设计随机游走的谱间隙。结合类随机限制方法，对随机选取变量集的不同赋值分别学习不同子函数，并使用在特殊设计特征空间上的Perceptron算法。

Result: 对任意常数γ≥1，学习规模n^γ的深度三电路，运行时间为2^{n-c_γ n}，c_γ>0仅依赖γ；实现了对任何一种多项式规模深度三电路相比朴素方法的指数级（多项式指数节省）改进。简化预热实例达到c_γ=exp(-O(γ))，进一步优化后达到c_γ=Ω(1)/γ。

Conclusion: 该工作显著改进了[ST17]的结果，首次实现对任意多项式规模深度三电路的运行时间达到2^{n-c_γ n}形式，其关键在于宽度-k CNF近似度界的改进以及随机限制与Perceptron结合的技术路线。

Abstract: We study the challenging problem of learning depth-three circuits in the mistake-bound model of (realizable) online learning, which is a more difficult model than distribution-free PAC learning. Prior algorithms for this problem, due to Servedio and Tan [ST17], could only learn polynomial-size depth-three circuits of poly$(n)$ size over $\{0,1\}^n$ with a running time of $2^{n - Ω(n/\log n)}$, and hence they ran in time $N^{1-o(1)}$ where $N=2^n$ is the running time of a naive memorization-based approach.
  In this work we substantially improve on the [ST17] result: for any constant $γ\geq1$, we give an algorithm that learns depth-three circuits of size $n^γ$ with running time \[ 2^{n-c_γn}, \] where $c_γ>0$ depends only on $γ$ and not on $n$. Hence we achieve a polynomial savings over the naive approach for learning any polynomial-size depth-three circuit.
  The main driving force behind our improvement is an improved bound on the approximate degree of width-$k$ CNFs. Inspired by Szegedy [Sze04] and Magniez et al. [MNRS11], the rough idea of our construction is to use a Chebyshev polynomial to efficiently amplify the spectral gap of a carefully designed random walk. This is combined with a random-restriction-like approach to separately learn different subfunctions corresponding to different assignments to a randomly chosen set of variables, using the Perceptron algorithm over a specially designed feature space.
  A simplified warmup instantiation of our approach achieves $c_γ= \exp(-O(γ))$; by augmenting this warmup with further ingredients we obtain the sharp form of our result, which achieves $c_γ=Ω(1)/γ$.

</details>


### [217] [Subquadratic-Query Algorithms for Finding Another Maximum Matroid Intersection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18199)
*Makoto Watanabe*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究给定两个拟阵独立神谕时判定并构造不同于已知最大公共独立集S的另一个最大公共独立集的问题，给出Las Vegas随机算法（期望Õ(n√r)次查询）与确定性算法（Õ(nr^{2/3})次查询），并应用于枚举所有最大公共独立集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在拟阵交中，已知一个最大公共独立集S后，判定是否存在另一个不同的最大公共独立集并输出之，是枚举与理解解空间的基础问题。已有方法通常需要构造交换图，代价较高。本文希望避免构造交换图，降低查询复杂度。

Method: 设M1、M2为同一基础集V上的两个拟阵，S为最大基数公共独立集，n=|V|，r=|S|。算法采用Kahn风格源剥离，在只删除的数据结构上进行，不构造交换图。一侧使用Blikstad等提出的重/轻分类；另一侧在无转置神谕时，给出集体随机分类器与确定性容量饱和证书。随机算法为Las Vegas，期望Õ(n√r)次独立查询；确定性算法为Õ(nr^{2/3})次查询。

Result: 得到两个算法：Las Vegas随机算法期望Õ(n√r)次独立神谕查询；确定性算法Õ(nr^{2/3})次查询。作为应用，可在相邻输出之间及最后一次输出后至多进行两次another-solution调用，从而枚举所有最大公共独立集；若解数为L，恰好进行2L-1次此类调用。

Conclusion: 该工作在不构造交换图的前提下，以更低的独立神谕查询复杂度解决了最大公共独立集的存在性判定与构造，并支持高效枚举所有最大公共独立集，兼具随机与确定性保证。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{M}_1,\mathcal{M}_2$ be two matroids on a common ground set $V$, given by independence oracles, and let $S$ be a maximum-cardinality common independent set. We study the problem of deciding whether there exists another maximum common independent set $T\ne S$, and of outputting one when it exists. Writing $n=|V|$ and $r=|S|$, we give a Las Vegas algorithm using $\tilde O(n\sqrt r)$ expected independence queries and a deterministic algorithm using $\tilde O(nr^{2/3})$ queries. As an application, all maximum common independent sets can be enumerated with at most two another-solution calls between consecutive outputs and after the last output; if there are $L$ solutions, exactly $2L-1$ such calls are made. Neither algorithm constructs the exchange graph. Instead, they perform Kahn-style source peeling through two deletion-only data structures. One side uses the heavy/light categorization of Blikstad, van den Brand, Mukhopadhyay, and Nanongkai. For the opposite side, where no transposed oracle is available, we give a collective randomized classifier and a deterministic capacity-saturation certificate.

</details>


### [218] [The Complexity of Undirected Partizan Edge Geography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18330)
*Yuto Okada*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了无向图上 Partizan Edge Geography 博弈的胜负判定是 PSPACE-完全的，即使限制在最大度为 3 的二部无向图上；同一归约也适用于两棋子不可共点的变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fraenkel 与 Simonson（TCS, 1993）已证明有向图上该博弈胜负判定为 PSPACE-完全，但无向图上的复杂度此前未解决，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过构造多项式时间归约，将已知 PSPACE-完全问题归约到最大度为 3 的二部无向图上的 Partizan Edge Geography 胜负判定问题；该归约同时处理两棋子不可共点的变体。

Result: 证明无向图上 Partizan Edge Geography 的胜负判定是 PSPACE-完全的，且该困难性在最大度为 3 的二部无向图中仍然成立；归约对两棋子不可共点变体同样有效。

Conclusion: 无向图上的 Partizan Edge Geography 与有向图情形同样困难，均为 PSPACE-完全，且在两棋子不可共点变体下结论不变。

Abstract: Partizan Edge Geography is a two-player game on a graph where each player has their own token on a vertex and moves their token to a neighbor in a turn removing the edge. Two player alternately move their tokens and the first player who cannot move loses the game. Fraenkel and Simonson (TCS, 1993) showed that the winner determination of this game is PSPACE-complete on directed graphs, given a graph and token positions.
  This paper resolves its complexity on undirected graphs by showing the PSPACE-completeness on bipartite undirected graphs of maximum degree 3. The same reduction also works for a variant where two tokens cannot be placed on the same vertex.

</details>


### [219] [NP-Hardness and a Fixed-Parameter Algorithm for Translocation Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18397)
*Maria Constantin,Adrian Miclăuş,Alexandru Popa*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究基因组重排中的易位（translocation）事件，定义了连续/非连续、均匀/非均匀易位及易位距离，证明了非均匀连续和非均匀非连续易位距离问题在任意有限字母表上均为NP难，并针对单目标串情形给出以目标串长度为参数的固定参数可 tractable 算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因组重排自1936年起被用于度量生物间进化距离。易位作为交换两条染色体前缀的操作，是基因组重排的重要形式，但非均匀易位距离问题的计算复杂性尚未明确，本文旨在刻画其难度并给出可算法化的特例。

Method: 将染色体表示为DNA字符串，定义易位操作为两条字符串间前缀的交换，产生两条新串并可继续参与后续易位。区分连续（新串单拷贝）与非连续（无限拷贝）、均匀（交换前缀等长）与非均匀（前缀不等长）易位。在此模型下定义输入串集合到目标串集合的易位距离为所需最少易位数，并分析其计算复杂性。

Result: 证明了非均匀连续易位距离问题和非均匀非连续易位距离问题在字母表为输入一部分的任意有限字母表上均是NP难的。对于目标集合仅含单个字符串的情形，给出了以目标串长度为参数的固定参数 tractable（FPT）算法。

Conclusion: 非均匀易位距离问题在一般情况下具有NP难的计算复杂性，但通过固定参数化方法，在单目标串这一受限情形下可实现高效求解，为基因组重排距离的计算提供了复杂性边界与算法途径。

Abstract: In this paper we study the genome rearrangements done by translocation events. Genome rearrangements were used to measure evolutionary distance between organisms since 1936 (Dobzhansky and Sturtevant). The chromosomes are represented as strings of DNA and the \emph{translocation operation} is defined as the exchange of prefixes between two strings. This operation results in the creation of two new strings (chromosomes) that can then be utilized in subsequent translocations. A translocation is referred to as \emph{contiguous} if the new strings are produced in a single copy, so each of them can be used in only one subsequent operation. When the words produced by a translocation operation are considered to have an infinite number of copies, the translocation is referred to as \emph{non-contiguous}. If the exchanged prefixes are of equal length, the translocation is called \emph{uniform}. Otherwise, the translocation is termed \emph{non-uniform}. The \emph{translocation distance} between two sets of strings, termed the input set and the target set, represents the minimum number of translocations necessary to obtain all the strings in the target set via translocation operations.
  We prove that both the non-uniform contiguous and the non-uniform non-contiguous translocation distance problems are NP-hard over arbitrary finite alphabets, where the alphabet is part of the input. For the case in which the target set consists of a single string, we give a fixed-parameter tractable algorithm parameterized by the length of the target string.

</details>


### [220] [Deterministic Streaming Lower Bounds for Approximate Maximum Clique and Maximum Independent Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18635)
*Adithya Diddapur*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究单遍边到达流模型下最大团与最大独立集问题的确定性算法空间复杂度，证明任何确定性算法需要 Ω(n²/(β·log n)) 比特空间，几乎匹配上界，从而解决了确定性与随机化之间的空间差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作给出使用 Õ(n²/β²) 比特空间的随机化算法及相应下界，似乎已解决该问题；但该算法依赖随机性，确定性最优算法仍为 O(n²/β) 比特的 folklore 去随机化，存在 Õ(β) 的确定性差距。作者旨在关闭这一差距。

Method: 通过两方单向通信（two-party one-way communication）下界证明，导出确定性算法的空间下界。

Result: 证明任何求解最大团或最大独立集的确定性算法必须使用 Ω(n²/(β·log n)) 比特空间，与已知确定性上界 O(n²/β) 几乎匹配（差距仅 O(log n) 因子）。

Conclusion: 该下界（几乎）紧地解决了确定性空间复杂度问题，并凸显了随机性在求解这两类问题中的威力。

Abstract: We study the canonical \textsf{Maximum Clique} and \textsf{Maximum Independent Set} problems in the one-pass edge-arrival graph streaming setting. Here, the edges of some input graph $G = (V,E)$ are presented one at a time (possibly including deletions), before an algorithm needs to produce either a large clique or independent set at the end of the stream, with the focus being on space complexity. We are interested in finding $β$-approximate solutions, for any $β\geq 1$.
  Previous work gave an algorithm using $\tilde{O}\left(n^2/β^2\right)$ bits of space, together with a corresponding $\tildeΩ\left(n^2/β^2\right)$ two-party communication lower bound [Halldórsson et al., ICALP'12], seeming to resolve the problem. However, their algorithm crucially relies on randomness, and the best known deterministic algorithm remains a folklore derandomisation using $O\left(n^2/β\right)$ bits of space, leaving a (deterministic) gap of size $\tilde{O}(β)$.
  We resolve this deterministic gap with an (almost) tight lower bound: any deterministic algorithm for either problem must use $Ω\left(\frac{n^2}{β\cdot\log n}\right)$ bits of space. Our proof is via a two-party one-way communication lower bound, and highlights the power of randomness when approaching either of these problems.

</details>


### [221] [Total Variation Distance Estimation through Domain Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18707)
*Arnab Bhattacharyya,Graham Cormode,Yucheng Fu,Kuldeep S. Meel*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文为积分布混合及一类结构化概率电路之间的全变差距离给出了首个 FPRAS，核心是借助 Lewis-weight 采样进行域约简。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简洁表示的高维分布之间的全变差距离计算通常是难解的，缺少在维度与混合分量数上均为多项式时间的有效近似算法。

Method: 提出域约简技术：对由赋值索引的特征向量族，用 Lewis-weight 采样将赋值域替换为多项式规模的加权子集，使该子集同时逼近每个线性投影的绝对值之和；对积分布混合，按坐标增量构造约简域；并将该方法推广到具有共同结构化架构的平滑、确定性、结构化可分解概率电路。

Result: 得到积分布混合间全变差距离的首个 FPRAS，运行时间在维度与混合分量数上均为多项式；上述方法进一步适用于更一般的结构化概率电路类别。

Conclusion: 域约简与 Lewis-weight 采样为高维分布全变差距离的近似计算提供了统一且高效的框架，可覆盖积分布混合及具有共同结构化架构的概率电路。

Abstract: Computing the total variation (TV) distance between succinctly represented high-dimensional distributions is generally intractable. We give an FPRAS for TV distance between mixtures of product distributions and, more generally, for a natural class of structured probabilistic circuits.
  Our main technique is a novel application of domain reduction: Given a family of feature vectors indexed by assignments, we use Lewis-weight sampling to replace the assignment domain by a polynomial-size weighted subset that simultaneously approximates the sum of absolute values of every linear projection. For mixtures of product distributions, we construct such reduced domains incrementally over the coordinates, obtaining the first FPRAS with running time polynomial in both the dimension and the number of mixture components. We then extend the approach to smooth, deterministic, structured-decomposable probabilistic circuits with a common structured architecture.

</details>


### [222] [A sampling Lovász Local Lemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18712)
*Dimitris Achlioptas*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文给出了满足 $4\mathrm{e}p(\Delta+1)^2\le1$ 条件约束满足问题(CSP)的近似均匀采样器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足特定条件的约束满足问题(CSP)提供近似均匀采样器,以在依赖图最大度数约束下实现高效采样。

Method: 算法将 Liu、Wang、Yin、Zhang、Zhou 近期提出的高效近似计数算法作为子程序调用。

Result: 在 $(n+m/\varepsilon)^{O(k\Delta\log D)}$ 时间内,返回与均匀分布总变差距离不超过 $\varepsilon$ 的满足赋值;其中 $n$、$m$ 分别为变量数与约束数,$D$ 为公共定义域大小,$k$ 为约束元数上界。

Conclusion: 该工作为满足 $4\mathrm{e}p(\Delta+1)^2\le1$ 的 CSP 提供了近似均匀采样方案,其复杂度由 $k$、$\Delta$、$\log D$ 等参数控制。

Abstract: We give an approximately uniform sampler for satisfying assignments of constraint satisfaction problems that satisfy $4\mathrm e p(Δ+1)^2\le1$, where $p$ is the largest constraint-violation probability under the uniform product distribution, and $Δ$ is the maximum degree of the dependency graph. The algorithm invokes the recent efficient approximate counting algorithm of Liu, Wang, Yin, Zhang, and Zhou as a subroutine and returns a satisfying assignment sampled within total-variation distance $\varepsilon$ of the uniform distribution in $(n+m/\varepsilon)^{O(kΔ\log D)}$ time, where $n$ and $m$ are the numbers of variables and constraints, $D$ is the common domain size, and $k$ bounds the constraint arity.

</details>


### [223] [Routing Multiple Agents Below the Sum of Distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18802)
*Matthias Bentert,Eduard Eiben,Fedor V. Fomin,Petr A. Golovach*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究瞬态多智能体路径规划（TMAP）问题，在图 G 中将智能体从起点无碰撞地路径规划至终点。采用 above-and-below-guarantee 参数化框架，以间隙 ζ = L − λ（其中 L 为各智能体最短路径距离之和的上界，λ 为目标完工时间）与智能体数 k 为参数，证明对该组合参数固定参数可解（FPT），并给出相应下界，基本刻画了该问题的参数化复杂度全景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典多智能体路径规划（MAPF）问题在一般情况下计算困难。作者引入瞬态 MAPF（TMAP）变体，并希望从参数化复杂度角度精确刻画其可解性边界。自然的参数 L（顺序路由下各智能体最短路径距离之和）通常很大，直接参数化意义有限；因此作者关注 L 与实际最优完工时间 λ 之间的间隙 ζ = L − λ，考察 k 与 ζ 组合下的参数化复杂度，以填补该问题的复杂度刻画空白。

Method: 在 above-and-below-guarantee 参数化范式下分析 TMAP：定义上界 L 为各智能体终端对之间最短路径距离之和（对应顺序路由），下界性质参数为目标完工时间 λ，参数化对象为间隙 ζ = L − λ 与智能体数 k。通过设计固定参数算法处理组合参数 k + ζ，并借助 W[1]-硬度归约与核（kernel）下界技术分析单一参数及组合参数的可解性与下界。

Result: 主要结果证明 TMAP 对组合参数 k + ζ 是固定参数可解的（FPT）。下界方面：仅以 k 为参数是 W[1]-难的；当终端不要求互异时，仅以 ζ 为参数也是 W[1]-难的。正面结果：若所有终端互异，则仅以 ζ 为参数即可固定参数可解。此外，证明 TMAP 以 k + ζ 为参数时不太可能存在多项式核。

Conclusion: 这些结果几乎完整地刻画了瞬态多智能体路径规划在参数 k、ζ 及组合 k + ζ 下的参数化复杂度全景：组合参数 k + ζ 下 FPT 可解，而单一参数 k 或（终端可重复时的）ζ 下 W[1]-难，终端互异时 ζ 单独可解，且不存在多项式核（在常规假设下）。

Abstract: We study Transient Multiagent Pathfinding, a variant of the classical Multi-Agent Pathfinding problem in which a set of agents must be routed without collisions from designated start vertices to designated destination vertices in a graph.
  We analyze the problem within the above-and-below-guarantee paradigm of parameterized complexity. In particular, we consider the natural upper bound \(L\), given by the sum of the shortest-path distances between pairs of agents' terminals (corresponding to sequential routing of the agents). The parameterization is given by the gap \(ζ= L - λ\) between this bound and the target makespan \(λ\), together with the number \(k\) of agents. Our main result establishes fixed-parameter tractability for the combined parameter \(k + ζ\).
  Matching lower bounds show that parameterization by \(k\) alone is W[1]-hard, and that parameterization by \(ζ\) alone is W[1]-hard when terminals are not required to be distinct. On the positive side, if all terminals are distinct, the problem becomes fixed-parameter tractable when parameterized solely by \(ζ\). Finally, we show that Transient Multiagent Pathfinding is unlikely to admit a polynomial kernel when parameterized by \(k + ζ\). Together, our results provide an almost complete characterization of the parameterized complexity landscape of the problem for the considered parameters.

</details>


### [224] [Optimizing Both Checking and Update Costs in Random Walk Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18833)
*Simon Apers,Marin Costes*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文给出了随机游走搜索中同时优化更新次数与检查次数的经典证明，通过平均化游走和耦合分析达到Dohotaru与Høyer提出的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机游走搜索中更新状态与检查标记的代价不同，已有两种策略分别最优化更新次数或检查次数，但尚缺乏同时匹配两者的经典证明。

Method: 将原始转移矩阵P替换为平均游走P̄_τ=1/τ∑_{k=1}^τ P^k，取τ为O(π(m)HT(m))量级，并利用与原游走的耦合及Kac引理进行分析。

Result: 证明平均游走在期望O(1/π(m))次检查内命中标记状态，总搜索代价期望为S + O(HT(m))U + O(1/π(m))C。

Conclusion: 该工作为任意不可约马尔可夫链提供了简短自包含的经典权衡证明，同时保证了更新与检查两方面的最优性。

Abstract: Random walks are a standard tool for search problems in which a state can be updated locally and tested for being marked. When updating the state and checking whether it is marked have different costs, two classical strategies optimize different parts of the cost: checking after every step is optimal in the number of updates, while repeatedly checking only after mixing is optimal in the number of checks. For a single marked state $m$ and a walk started from its stationary distribution $π$, Dohotaru and Høyer stated that both guarantees can be matched simultaneously, for a walk that checks after blocks of a fixed length; their argument is sketched through quantum walks, and they observe that they know of no classical proof. We give a short and self-contained classical proof of such a tradeoff, for arbitrary irreducible Markov chains. The algorithm replaces the original transition matrix $P$ by the averaged walk $
  \overline P_τ= \frac{1}τ\sum_{k=1}^τ P^k, $ where $τ$ is of order $π(m)HT(m)$. Using a coupling with the original walk and Kac's lemma, we prove directly that the averaged walk hits the marked state in $O(1/π(m))$ checks in expectation. The resulting search cost is \[
  S + O(HT(m))U + O(1/π(m))C \] in expectation, where $S$, $U$, and $C$ denote setup, update, and checking costs.

</details>


### [225] [Degree-Free Spectral Independence for Log-Concave Holant Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18835)
*Xiaoyu Chen,Zejia Chen,Xinyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文建立简单图上对数凹 Holant 问题的谱独立性的与度数无关的界，并据此给出单体-二聚体模型与 b-匹配 Glauber 动力学的弛豫时间界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有谱独立性与 Glauber 动力学混合时间结果常依赖图的最大度数，本文希望去除度数依赖，为对数凹 Holant 问题及匹配类模型给出仅随边数 m 线性增长的弛豫时间界。

Method: 证明简单图上对数凹 Holant 问题的谱独立性满足与图度数无关的界；将该界应用于单体-二聚体模型和 b-匹配模型，分析其 Glauber 动力学的弛豫时间，并对均匀 b-匹配做进一步优化。主要证明思路借助 GPT-5.6 Sol 发现。

Result: 活动度 λ 下单体-二聚体模型的弛豫时间界为 O_λ(m)；fugacity λ>0 下 b-匹配的弛豫时间界为 O_{b,λ}(m)；均匀 b-匹配的弛豫时间界改进为 O(bm)，其中 m 为边数。

Conclusion: 对数凹 Holant 问题具有与度数无关的谱独立性，由此得到的匹配类模型 Glauber 动力学弛豫时间为边数的近线性量级，且均匀 b-匹配可进一步达到 O(bm)。

Abstract: We establish a degree-independent bound on spectral independence for log-concave Holant problems on simple graphs. As a corollary, we obtain relaxation-time bounds for Glauber dynamics of $O_λ(m)$ for the monomer-dimer model at activity $λ$ and $O_{b,λ}(m)$ for $b$-matchings at fugacity $λ>0$, where $m$ is the number of edges. For uniform $b$-matchings, the relaxation-time bound improves to $O(bm)$. The main proof ideas were found using GPT-5.6 Sol.

</details>


### [226] [A Walk From Free Probability to Matrix Discrepancy II: Weaver's Problem and the Kadison-Singer Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18913)
*Tarun Kathuria*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文在实算术模型下给出确定性多项式时间算法，求解一般输入的Weaver偏差问题，偏差至多35√ε。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Weaver偏差结果的既有证明是存在性的，解决了Kadison–Singer猜想，但对一般输入高效找到符号仍是开放算法问题。

Method: 算法从超立方体原点走向顶点，坐标触面即固定；势函数度量受算子值自由半圆元扰动的偏差矩阵的软谱边缘。结合Lehner变分公式与谱Tsallis-1/2正则化，并借助自由插值思路，得到有限维SDP形式。通过正则化min–max问题的线性化KKT系统分析最优解稳定性，其驻点方程关联矩阵Dyson方程，从而给出移动规则。

Result: 得到确定性多项式时间算法，偏差至多35√ε；势函数有有限维SDP表述，可同时分析偏差与剩余协方差；移动规则保证选择更优符号控制偏差并增大到原点的平方距离。

Conclusion: 该工作将Weaver偏差的存在性结果算法化，并提供基于自由概率、SDP与KKT稳定性的分析框架；后续工作将处理高阶Kadison–Singer与谱稀疏树，主要偏差定理的Lean形式化已完成。

Abstract: \cite{mss2015} proved Weaver's discrepancy result existentially, resolving the Kadison--Singer conjecture . Finding such signs efficiently for general inputs remained an open algorithmic question. In the real-arithmetic model, we give a deterministic algorithm running in polynomial time with discrepancy at most $35\sqrt\varepsilon$.
  The algorithm walks from the origin of the hypercube to a vertex, fixing coordinates as they hit a face. Its potential measures a soft spectral edge of the discrepancy matrix perturbed by an operator-valued free semicircular element. The perturbation's covariance vanishes as the coefficients reach their endpoints. Inspired by the free interpolation approach of Bandeira, Boedihardjo, and van Handel \cite{bbvh2023}, we combine Lehner's variational formula \cite{lehner1999} with spectral Tsallis--$1/2$ regularization used in \cite{allenZhuLiaoOrecchia2015} and \cite{pesentivladu2026}. The resulting potential has a finite-dimensional SDP formulation, allowing the discrepancy and remaining covariance to be analyzed together.
  We analyze the optimizer's stability through the linearized Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) system of a regularized min--max problem, whose stationarity equations are related to the matrix Dyson equation \cite{erdos2019}. This gives the movement rule: either a coordinate can move toward its nearer endpoint at small spectral cost, or a low-curvature direction orthogonal to the current coefficient vector allows further progress. Choosing the better sign of this direction controls discrepancy while increasing the squared distance from the origin. Upcoming work \cite{kathuria2026higherRank} will address higher-rank Kadison-Singer and spectrally thin trees. Lean formalizations of our main discrepancy theorems have been completed and will be released shortly.

</details>


### [227] [A Walk From Free Probability to Matrix Discrepancy I: Matrix Spencer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18914)
*Tarun Kathuria*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文证明矩阵Spencer猜想：对算子范数不超过1的实对称矩阵组，存在签名使符号和的算子范数不超过O(√(n log(2m/n)))，并给出多项式时间的随机算法，同时在实数算术模型中处理方形与矩形情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵Spencer猜想是差异理论中的核心问题，此前仅有非算法性存在性结果，缺乏高效构造性证明。本文旨在给出该界的随机多项式时间算法，并从方形推广到矩形矩阵情形。

Method: 采用协方差控制的超立方体随机游走，从原点出发，逐步对接近面的坐标进行舍入并固定。势函数刻画演化差异矩阵被算子值自由半圆元扰动后的软谱边缘，结合Lehner自由边缘变分公式与谱Tsallis正则化，将差异与剩余协方差统一为光滑优化问题，并具有有限维半正定表述；其优化器的稳定性由与矩阵Dyson方程相关的方程控制，从而找到可移动的大子空间。方形情形用Tsallis-1/2正则化，矩形情形用广义Tsallis幂正则化。

Result: 证明了m≤n时的O(√n)界，解决了方形情形；通过更换正则化器得到矩形界，即达到O(√(n log(2m/n)))的算子范数上界，算法在实数算术模型下具有多项式运行时间。

Conclusion: 该工作为矩阵Spencer猜想提供了算法性证明，建立了自由概率与差异理论方法之间的联系，其伴生论文将该思想用于Weaver差异定理的算法证明；主要差异定理的Lean形式化已完成并将发布。

Abstract: The Matrix Spencer conjecture asks whether any $n$ real symmetric matrices A_1,...,A_n \in \mathbb{R}^{m \times m} of operator norm at most one admit a signing $x\in\{-1,1\}^n$ such that the operator norm of the signed sum is at most O(\sqrt{n \log(2m/n)}) We give a randomized algorithm establishing this bound with polynomial runtime in the real-arithmetic model. We first prove the $O(\sqrt n)$ bound for $m\le n$, resolving the square case, and then obtain the rectangular bound by changing the regularizer. As in earlier algorithmic discrepancy methods \cite{lovettmeka2012,bansalLaddhaVempala2022,pesentivladu2026}, we run a covariance-controlled random walk from the origin of the hypercube, rounding coordinates near its faces and keeping them fixed. Our potential measures a soft spectral edge of the evolving discrepancy matrix perturbed by an operator-valued free semicircular element. Inspired by the free interpolation approach of \cite{bbvh2023}, we combine Lehner's variational formula for the free edge \cite{lehner1999} with spectral Tsallis regularization \cite{allenZhuLiaoOrecchia2015,pesentivladu2026}. This puts the discrepancy and remaining covariance in a single smooth optimization problem. The potential has a finite-dimensional semidefinite formulation. Stability of its optimizer, governed by equations related to the matrix Dyson equation \cite{erdos2019}, lets us find a large subspace in which to move while controlling discrepancy. The square case uses the Tsallis--$1/2$ regularizer; the rectangular case uses a suitable generalized Tsallis power regularizer.
  Our companion paper \cite{kathuria2026ks} applies these ideas to give an algorithmic proof of Weaver's discrepancy theorem, whose existence proof by [MSS15] resolved the Kadison--Singer conjecture \cite{mss2015}.Lean formalizations of our main discrepancy theorems have been completed and will be released shortly.

</details>


### [228] [Constant Factor Optimal 2-Resilient Local Failover Routing Scheme on Directed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18990)
*Kaito Harada,Naoki Kitamura,Yuki Kawashima,Yuya Terashima*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究了有向图上容忍最多 k 条弧失效的局部故障切换路由，用可重写头部位数衡量效率，给出改进的上界与下界，k=2 时上下界相差至多 3 比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部故障切换路由只需预计算的逐节点转发规则和少量可重写头部即可保证 s 到 t 交付，且能容忍网络至多 k 条弧失效。本文聚焦 k≥2（此前研究多集中于 k=1），目标是用更少可重写比特提升效率，并缩小上下界差距。

Method: 上界方面：设计逐节点转发方案，对一般 k 使用至多 ⌈log C(2n+k-4, k-1)⌉+1 比特；对 k=2 进一步设计至多 ⌈log(1+2⌊2(n-1)/3⌋)⌉ 比特的方案。下界方面：提出新的路径小部件（path-gadget）构造，导出改进下界：k=2 时为 ⌈log⌊n/3⌋⌉ 比特；一般 k（记 k2=⌊k/2⌋）时为 ⌈k2 log⌊(n-1+k2)/(3k2)⌋⌉ 比特。

Result: 一般 k 的上界 ⌈log C(2n+k-4, k-1)⌉+1 在任意 k<2n-3 时均优于此前最优的 ⌈log C(2n+k-3, k)⌉；k=2 上界改进为 ⌈log(1+2⌊2(n-1)/3⌋)⌉。下界方面，k=2 得 ⌈log⌊n/3⌋⌉，一般 k 得 ⌈k2 log⌊(n-1+k2)/(3k2)⌋⌉，均改进此前最优下界。特别地，k=2 时上界与下界相差至多 3 比特。

Conclusion: 本文为有向图局部故障切换路由在 k≥2 情形提供了更紧的头部比特复杂度刻画：给出改进的上界方案与基于路径小部件的新下界，显著缩小了 k=2 时的上下界间隙（≤3 比特），推进了该问题的理解。

Abstract: Local failover routing delivers a packet from a source $s$ to a destination $t$ using only pre-computed, per-node forwarding rules together with a small rewritable packet header, remaining correct even when up to $k$ arcs of the network fail. We study this problem on directed graphs with $n$ nodes, measuring efficiency by the number of rewritable bits carried in the header, and focus on the case of $k \ge 2$ failures. Our first contribution is an improved upper bound for general $k$. We design a scheme using at most $\lceil \log\!\binom{2n+k-4}{k-1} \rceil+1$ bits, improving the best previously known $\lceil \log \binom{2n+k-3}{k} \rceil$ bit scheme for every $k < 2n-3$. For the special case $k=2$, we further improve this to a scheme using at most $\left\lceil \log\!\left(1+2\lfloor 2(n-1)/3 \rfloor\right)\right\rceil$ bits. Our second contribution is a family of improved lower bounds, obtained via a new path-gadget construction. For $k=2$, we prove a lower bound of $\left\lceil \log \lfloor n/3 \rfloor \right\rceil$ bits, and for general $k$, writing $k_2 = \lfloor k/2 \rfloor$, a lower bound of $\left\lceil k_2 \log \left\lfloor (n-1+k_2)/(3k_2) \right\rfloor \right\rceil$ bits, improving on the best previously known bound. In particular, for $k=2$, our upper bound of $\left\lceil \log\!\left(1+2\lfloor 2(n-1)/3 \rfloor\right)\right\rceil$ bits and our lower bound of $\left\lceil \log \lfloor n/3 \rfloor \right\rceil$ bits differ by at most $3$ bits.

</details>


### [229] [Deterministic online matching under short augmenting paths and restricted vertex reassignments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19001)
*Mariano Llancamán,José A. Soto*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究带局部补救的确定性在线二分匹配问题，提出 Lowest-Cost-Path (LCP) 算法，在任意预算组合下达到最优竞争比，并给出匹配的确定性上界；同时刻画了两个离线加权变体的精确最优比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线二分匹配中，允许有限次数的重新分配（局部补救）有望提升匹配质量，但既有工作缺乏对任意补救预算下确定性最优竞争比的完整刻画，也缺乏对加权变体的精确分析。本文旨在填补这一空白，明确局部补救（长度 1 或 3 的增广路）在两个维度预算约束下能达到的极限性能。

Method: 在预算化模型 OMP_3(s,t) 中（每个离线顶点最多被重分配 s 次，每个在线顶点最多 t 次），提出确定性算法 Lowest-Cost-Path (LCP)。分析采用原始—对偶（primal–dual）技术，并借助 LCP 历史图的结构性刻画，说明耗尽的重新分配预算如何阻断长度为 3 的增广。此外分析了离线加权变体 WOMP_3，针对单位离线预算情形求解精确确定性竞争比。

Result: LCP 对每个预算组合均达到最优竞争比：当 s=0 或 t=0 时为 1/2；对任意 s≥1 与有限 t，最优比为 γ_t=(2·2^t−1)/(3·2^t−1)，其中 t=1 时为 3/5，随 t 增大收敛至 2/3；t=∞ 时恰为 2/3。即每个离线顶点仅允许一次重分配即可达到与任意更大离线预算相同的最优保证。对所有确定性算法给出了匹配的上界。加权变体中：WOMP_3(1,1) 的最优比为 2−√2，WOMP_3(1,∞) 为 (√5−1)/2，均严格低于对应的无权情形；若权重置于在线顶点，则不存在具有正竞争比的确定性算法。

Conclusion: 局部补救（长度 1 或 3 的增广）在极小预算下即可逼近无权在线二分匹配的理论上界 2/3，且离线侧的单次重分配已充分；离线加权会降低最优竞争比，而在线加权则完全破坏确定性算法的可实现保证，揭示了权重位置对竞争性能的决定性影响。

Abstract: We study deterministic online bipartite matching with local recourse. Online vertices arrive one by one and reveal edges to a fixed offline set. After each arrival, the algorithm maintains a matching and may update it only by an augmenting path of length $1$ or $3$ starting at the new vertex. In the budgeted model $\operatorname{OMP}_3(s,t)$, each offline vertex can be reassigned at most $s$ times and each online vertex at most $t$ times.
  Our main result is a deterministic algorithm, Lowest-Cost-Path ($\operatorname{LCP}$), that achieves the optimal competitive ratio for every choice of budgets. If $s=0$ or $t=0$, the best possible ratio is $1/2$. For every $s\ge 1$ and finite $t$, the optimal ratio is $$ γ_t=\frac{2\cdot 2^t-1}{3\cdot 2^t-1}. $$ This value equals $3/5$ for $t=1$ and converges to $2/3$ as $t$ increases. For $t=\infty$, the optimal ratio is exactly $2/3$. In particular, allowing a single reassignment per offline vertex already matches the best guarantee achievable with any larger offline budget. We also prove matching upper bounds for all deterministic algorithms.
  Our analysis is primal--dual and uses a structural description of the history graph of $\operatorname{LCP}$, which explains how exhausted reassignment budgets can block length-$3$ augmentations. We also study offline-weighted variants. In particular, we determine the exact optimal deterministic ratios for two unit-offline-budget cases: $2-\sqrt2$ for $\operatorname{WOMP}_3(1,1)$, and $(\sqrt5-1)/2$ for $\operatorname{WOMP}_3(1,\infty)$. Both are strictly below their unweighted counterparts. If weights are placed on online vertices instead, no deterministic algorithm has a positive competitive ratio.

</details>


### [230] [Secretary Problems with Interactive Ordinal Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19016)
*José A. Soto,Felipe Valdevenito*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究带有限序数查询预言机的秘书问题，在单选择秘书问题中针对随机序、对抗序及两种基于样本的对抗序，分别无查询、一次查询和无限查询情形给出紧界；并将模型推广至拟阵秘书问题，通过简单查询与完全查询两种接口得到概率竞争比结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典秘书问题中算法只能观测到达元素的相对序，决策不可撤销，成功率受信息限制。作者希望探索：若允许在线算法向可靠预言机提出有限次序数查询（不揭示数值，仅回答相对于已观测信息的问题），能否提升成功率，并在不同到达顺序模型下刻画查询能力与最优竞争比之间的关系。

Method: 将预言机查询定义为序数型：单选择情形中一次查询返回超过当前记录的未见元素数量；分析随机序、对抗序和两种基于样本的对抗序，在零次、一次和无限次查询下的最优成功概率。进一步将模型扩展到拟阵秘书问题，设计两种查询接口：简单查询识别可单独改进当前最优解的未见元素，完全查询给出未见元素相对于当前最优权的更精细序数划分；在基于样本的在线对抗序与随机序下分析概率竞争算法。

Result: 单选择秘书问题中除一个变体外均获紧界：对抗序下查询不优于平凡的1/n保证；随机序下一次查询将最优成功概率从1/e提升至0.4477…，无限查询达到最优1/2；基于样本模型中，在线对抗序下一次查询已达紧值1/e，离线对抗序下无限查询给出精确值(20√10−29)/81，剩余一次查询离线对抗情形开放，并给出两个部分上界。拟阵秘书问题中，基于样本在线对抗序下单次简单查询得到1/4概率竞争算法，完全查询提升至最优1/e；随机序下无限完全查询达到最优1/2概率竞争比。

Conclusion: 有限序数查询能显著增强在线秘书算法的能力，但其增益高度依赖到达顺序模型与查询接口：对抗序下收益有限，随机序与样本模型下可逼近甚至达到最优竞争比。拟阵推广表明更丰富的序数划分可将竞争比从1/4提升至1/e乃至1/2。一个单选择离线对抗一次查询问题仍未解决。

Abstract: We study secretary problems in which the online algorithm can ask a reliable oracle a limited number of ordinal queries about unseen elements. Values are revealed only when elements arrive, and all accept/reject decisions are irrevocable. The oracle never reveals numerical values; it only answers questions defined relative to the information already observed by the algorithm.
  We first consider the single-choice secretary problem. A query returns the number of unseen elements whose value exceeds the current record. We analyze random order, adversarial order, and two sample-based adversarial orders, with no queries, one query, and unlimited queries. We obtain tight bounds for all variants except one. In adversarial order, queries do not improve over the trivial $1/n$ guarantee. In random order, one query improves the optimal success probability from $1/e$ to $0.4477\ldots$, and unlimited queries achieve the optimal value $1/2$. In the sample-based models, one query already yields the tight value $1/e$ against an online adversary, while unlimited queries give the exact value $(20\sqrt{10}-29)/81$ against an offline adversary. The remaining one-query offline-adversarial case is left open, and we give two partial upper bounds for it.
  We also extend the model to the matroid secretary problem, where we study two ordinal query interfaces. A simple query identifies the unseen elements that would individually improve the current optimum on the observed set, while a complete query gives a finer ordinal partition of the unseen elements by weight relative to that optimum. Under sample-based online adversarial order, a single simple query yields a $1/4$ probability-competitive algorithm, and a complete query improves this to the optimal $1/e$. Finally, with unlimited complete queries in the random-order model, we achieve the optimal $1/2$ probability-competitive ratio.

</details>


### [231] [A Near-Optimal Space Lower Bound for Euclidean Diameter Estimation in Dynamic Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19089)
*Ashwin Padaki,Krish Singal,Erik Waingarten*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究动态（turnstile）流模型中欧氏空间点集直径估计的空间复杂度，证明了与Indyk上界本质匹配的下界：任何能给出c-近似的动态流算法必须使用n^{Õ(1/c²)}空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Indyk (SODA 2003)给出了使用n^{O(1/c²)}空间对n个向量欧氏直径做c-近似的上界，但该问题的空间复杂度下界长期未明。本文旨在填补这一空白，证明与上界本质匹配的下界。

Method: 在动态（turnstile）流模型下研究欧氏距离直径估计问题，通过构造相应的下界证明技术（如通信复杂度或硬实例归约），针对c-近似算法建立空间下界。

Result: 证明了任何能够c-近似n个欧氏向量直径的动态流算法，必须使用n^{Õ(1/c²)}空间。

Conclusion: 欧氏直径估计的动态流空间复杂度已被基本确定：上界n^{O(1/c²)}与下界n^{Õ(1/c²)}匹配，该问题复杂度刻画完备。

Abstract: We study the space complexity of diameter estimation for a set of points in Euclidean space in the dynamic (turnstile) streaming model. The seminal work of Indyk (SODA 2003) gives a $c$-approximation to the Euclidean diameter of $n$ vectors using $n^{O(1/c^2)}$ space. Our main contribution is giving an essentially matching lower bound. Any dynamic streaming algorithm which can $c$-approximate the diameter of $n$ Euclidean vectors must use $n^{\tildeΩ(1/c^2)}$ space.

</details>


### [232] [On the Strong Matroid Secretary Conjecture and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19118)
*Hamed Abdi,Kiarash Banihashem,MohammadTaghi Hajiaghayi,Danny Mittal*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明线性拟阵满足强拟阵秘书猜想（给出1/e竞争比的序数秘书算法），并给出任意拟阵的1/2竞争比单样本预言机算法及相应的黑盒归约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强拟阵秘书猜想断言每个拟阵都存在1/e竞争比的秘书算法，但长期未解决。作者希望通过有限线性规划的数值证据（小规模拟阵最优比值超过1/e、截断单调性观察）来指导理论研究，并系统刻画拟阵秘书与单样本预言机两类问题的可达竞争比。

Method: 构造有限线性规划，其最优值等于固定拟阵的最优序数竞争比；对7元素全部正秩拟阵及几乎全部8元素拟阵求解，并检验截断单调性（对均匀拟阵证明、对某图拟阵反证）。据此对线性拟阵（含图拟阵、正则拟阵、层状拟阵、gammoid）构造1/e竞争比的序数秘书算法：维护已接受张成与各环境子空间期望交维数的界，用uncrossing与separation论证可在以规定概率接受当前贪心基元素的同时保持这些界。另给出单样本预言机算法：输出恰好等价于从新鲜乘积抽取的最优解的独立公平稀疏化，用O(n^2)次独立性查询；并给出将单样本预言机比α转为秘书比α^2/16的黑盒多项式归约。

Result: 对7元素全部正秩拟阵及几乎全部8元素拟阵，最优序数竞争比严格大于1/e；最优比值在均匀拟阵截断下随秩严格递增，但在某图拟阵截断下不单调（反例）。对每个线性拟阵得到1/e竞争比的序数秘书算法。任意拟阵得到竞争比1/2的单样本预言机算法（任意固定到达顺序、独立于样本与取值），查询复杂度O(n^2)，两个常数在各自模型下紧。归约将1/2的单样本比转化为1/64的秘书比，从而对任意拟阵得到1/64竞争比的序数秘书算法。

Conclusion: 强拟阵秘书猜想在线性拟阵这一广泛类上成立，1/e可达；数值规划与单调性分析为构造提供了有效指引。对一般拟阵，单样本预言机模型可获得紧的1/2比，并通过黑盒归约给出通用1/64竞争比秘书算法，明确了两类模型间的定量转化关系。

Abstract: The strong matroid secretary conjecture asserts that every matroid admits a $1/e$-competitive secretary algorithm, matching the classical single-choice guarantee. We formulate a finite linear program whose value is the optimal ordinal competitive ratio of any fixed matroid; for all matroids of positive rank on seven elements and nearly all on eight, this value exceeds $1/e$. The same computations suggested that the optimal ratio is monotone under truncation of the matroid; we prove this for uniform matroids, where the ratio is strictly increasing in the rank, and refute it for a graphic matroid. Guided by this evidence, we prove the conjecture for every linear matroid, a class that includes graphic matroids, regular matroids, laminar matroids, and gammoids, giving a $1/e$-competitive ordinal secretary algorithm. The algorithm maintains bounds on the expected intersection dimension of the accepted span with every ambient subspace. Uncrossing and separation show that these bounds can be preserved while admitting each current greedy-basis element with a prescribed probability and the construction uses finite linear programs.
  For every matroid, we also give a single-sample prophet algorithm with competitive ratio $1/2$ in any fixed arrival order independent of the samples and values. Its output, including the selected values, has exactly the law of an independent fair thinning of an optimum from a fresh product draw. The algorithm uses $O(n^2)$ independence queries on $n$ elements. Both constants are tight in their respective models. We also give a self-contained black-box reduction that converts a single-sample prophet ratio $α$ into a secretary ratio $α^2/16$, preserving polynomial running time. Our single-sample algorithm consequently yields a $1/64$-competitive ordinal secretary algorithm for arbitrary matroids.

</details>


### [233] [A $2$-Approximation for Directed Feedback Vertex Set in Locally Semicomplete and Quasi-Transitive Digraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19129)
*Sounak Modak*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究有向反馈顶点集（DFVS）问题，将锦标赛上的随机因子-2近似推广到局部半完全有向图（LSD）和拟传递有向图两类含稀疏图的结构化有向图类，均实现加权DFVS的随机多项式时间因子-2近似，且因子2在UGC下最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般DFVS在Unique Games Conjecture下不存在常数因子近似，锦标赛类有Lokshtanov等人的随机因子-2近似。作者希望将这一保证推广到更广泛、且包含稀疏有向图的结构化有向图类，填补LSD上非平凡常数因子近似的空白，并改进拟传递有向图上的近似比。

Method: 提出组合框架（composition framework）。首个主要结果：对局部半完全有向图（LSD）上的加权DFVS，给出随机多项式时间因子-2近似。第二个结果：对拟传递有向图的加权DFVS，通过对组合框架的简单递归应用，得到随机多项式时间因子-2近似。

Result: （1）LSD上加权DFVS的随机因子-2近似，为LSD上首个非平凡常数因子近似，即使在无权情形亦然；（2）拟传递有向图上加权DFVS的随机因子-2近似，改进了Ghorbani和Mnich [ICALP'26] 的确定性9/4近似。因子2在UGC下最优，因为锦标赛同时是LSD和拟传递有向图的子类。

Conclusion: 将锦标赛上DFVS的因子-2近似保证成功推广至LSD与拟传递有向图两类结构，因子2在UGC下不可改进。

Abstract: A \emph{directed feedback vertex set} of a digraph is a set of vertices whose removal destroys all directed cycles. The \textsc{Directed Feedback Vertex Set} (\textsc{DFVS}) problem asks for such a set of minimum cardinality or minimum total weight. Although general \textsc{DFVS} admits no constant-factor approximation under the {Unique Games Conjecture}, tournaments admit a randomized factor-$2$ approximation due to Lokshtanov et al. [SODA'20]. We extend this guarantee to two broader classes of structured digraphs, both of which also contain sparse digraphs.
  Our first and main result is a randomized polynomial-time factor-$2$ approximation for weighted \textsc{DFVS} on \emph{locally semicomplete digraphs} (\textsf{LSD}s), a class that strictly generalizes semicomplete digraphs and tournaments. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first non-trivial constant-factor approximation for \textsc{DFVS} on \textsf{LSD}s, even in the unweighted setting.
  Our second result is a randomized polynomial-time factor-$2$ approximation for weighted \textsc{DFVS} on \emph{quasi-transitive digraphs}, improving the recent deterministic $9/4$-approximation of Ghorbani and Mnich~[ICALP'26]. The algorithm follows from a simple recursive application of our composition framework.
  The factor $2$ is optimal under the {Unique Games Conjecture}, since tournaments are subclass of \textsf{LSD}s as well as quasi-transitive digraphs.

</details>


### [234] [Maximum Matching Size for Bounded Arboricity Graphs in the Dynamic Graph Stream Model using $\tilde{O}(n^{2/3})$ space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19136)
*Andrew McGregor*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出一种单遍插入-删除图流算法，对树萌度为α的图返回最大匹配大小的(1+ε)(α+2)-近似，空间复杂度为O(ε^{-4/3}α^{4/3}n^{2/3} polylog n)，将此前最优空间界从O(n^{4/5})改进至O(n^{2/3})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在图流模型中估计最大匹配大小是流算法领域的核心问题之一。已有最优单遍算法空间约为O(n^{4/5})，在高稀疏（小树萌度）图上有较大提升空间，因此作者希望在插入-删除流模型下降低空间需求，同时保持近似比可接受。

Method: 算法为单遍（one-pass）线性草图（linear sketch），针对树萌度不超过α的图。无需对删除次数或中间图树萌度做任何假设。核心思路是利用线性草图的可合并性与树萌度结构，在受限树萌度假设下压缩所需维护的信息量，从而以O(ε^{-4/3}α^{4/3}n^{2/3} polylog n)的空间实现(1+ε)(α+2)-近似估计。

Result: 对于常数α和ε，空间复杂度从先前最优的O(n^{4/5} polylog n)降低到O(n^{2/3} polylog n)。近似比达到(1+ε)(α+2)。算法为单遍线性草图，且对删除次数和中间图树萌度无额外约束。

Conclusion: 该工作显著推进了受限树萌度图流中最大匹配估计的空间下界理解，以线性草图的简洁形式实现了空间的大幅改进，并保持了对插入-删除流中动态删除的鲁棒性。

Abstract: The paper presents a one-pass algorithm in the insert-delete graph stream model that returns a $(1+\varepsilon)(α+2)$-approximation for the size of the maximum matching in a graph of arboricity at most $α$. The algorithm uses $O(\varepsilon^{-4/3}α^{4/3}n^{2/3} \text{polylog} n)$ space. For constant $α$ and $\varepsilon$, this improves the best known previous space bound from $O(n^{4/5} \text{polylog} n)$ to $O(n^{2/3} \text{polylog} n)$. The algorithm is a linear sketch and requires no bounds on the number of deletions or on the arboricity of intermediate graphs.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [235] [Making AI-Assisted Claims Independently Challengeable: Publication Authority and a Protocol for Falsifiable Publication Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17631)
*Torsten Olivi Tiltack,Yifei Dong,Kun Yu,Xu Wang,Wei Liu,Jianlong Zhou,Ren Ping Liu,Fang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"出版权威"(Publication Authority)概念,将其形式化为精确状态、不可转移、单次使用的出版能力,并在PAC-2026(AIJIM协议候选)中实现;经SF-4冻结规范的有限模型与历史实现验证,支持内部一致性、有界安全性与故障敏感性,但不支持事实真伪、通用精化、盲互操作性、现场有效性与标准地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助的学术主张在证据、分析、人类授权、呈现形式与更正历史各自指向不同状态时,仍可能显得权威可信。现有溯源、认证与透明度机制虽能暴露历史,却无法界定此处所考察的"出版状态转移"本身,因此需要一个可判定、不可转移的出版授权机制。

Method: 构建Publication Authority为精确状态、不可转移、单次使用的出版能力,并实例化为机器可读的PAC-2026(出版问责演算,AIJIM协议候选)。评估其第四个有界语义冻结SF-4(面向可替换绑定的固定剖面规范)。规范含六项义务:证据、运行与制品、测量披露、授权、表面一致性、生命周期连续性;每项义务产生目标绑定见证、局部反例或局部不可验证性,彼此不可相互补偿。仅当生成全新且完整的全通过记录,才导出供一次原子出版转移消耗的许可。验证采用身份向量、对抗案例、有限模型与历史实现。

Result: 十个模型探索了110,764个安全可达状态;76个不安全配置均产生预期的违规或观察者反模型。读者表面即使通过其一致性检查,也不能授权出版,除非被接受的记录承认该表面。SF-4将证据视野与验证时间分离,并拒绝真实但因果无效的授权。历史前驱路径复现了17个冻结的授权-后继结果。随后一项内部、实例盲的已知案例类测试与全部183项评分期望一致;同主机包执行复现了其240条存档观测。

Conclusion: 结果支持该机制的内部一致性、有界安全性、故障敏感性与有限可构造性,但不支持其事实真伪性、通用精化能力、盲互操作性、现场有效性或标准地位。

Abstract: AI-assisted claims can appear authoritative when evidence, analysis, human authorization, presentation, and correction history refer to different states. Provenance, attestation, and transparency expose history but alone do not specify the publication transition examined here. We develop Publication Authority as an exact-state, non-transferable, single-use publication capability and instantiate it in PAC-2026 (Publication-Accountability Calculus), a machine-readable AIJIM Protocol candidate. We evaluate its fourth bounded semantic freeze (SF-4), a fixed-profile specification designed for replaceable bindings. Six obligations govern evidence, runs and artifacts, measurement disclosure, authorization, surface correspondence, and lifecycle continuity. Each yields a target-bound witness, localized counterexample, or localized unverifiability; none can compensate for another. Only a fresh, complete all-pass record derives the permit consumed by one atomic publication transition. We use identity vectors, adversarial cases, finite models, and historical implementations. Ten models explored 110,764 safe reachable states; 76 unsafe configurations produced the expected violation or observer countermodel. A reader surface passing its correspondence check cannot authorize publication unless the accepted record admits that surface. SF-4 separates evidence horizon from verification time and rejects an authentic but causally invalid authorization. A historical predecessor path reproduced 17 frozen authorization-successor outcomes. A later in-house, instance-blind test of known case classes matched all 183 scored expectations; same-host package execution reproduced its 240 archived observations. Results support internal coherence, bounded safety, fault sensitivity, and limited constructibility, but not factual truth, general refinement, blind interoperability, field efficacy, or standards status.

</details>


### [236] [EvolveTrade: Experience-Driven Policy Refinement for Self-Evolving LLM Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17632)
*Sehee Kim,Yumin Choi,Minki Kang,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvolveTrade 将工具型交易代理的系统提示视为文本参数化策略，通过策略代理利用决策轨迹与组合反馈持续修改该策略，在固定主干 LLM 的前提下提升跨市场状态下的夏普比率与累计收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 交易代理多依赖部署前手写固定的工具使用策略，难以在市场状态变化时自适应调整证据收集、工具调用、信号验证与风险管理方式。

Method: 提出 EvolveTrade 自演化框架：把工具型交易代理的系统提示当作文本参数化策略；每个更新周期后，由 Policy Agent 基于累积的决策轨迹与已实现组合反馈修订该策略，主干 LLM 保持冻结；更新后的策略用于下一批交易决策，从而迭代优化信息获取与组合构建流程。

Result: 在多种市场状态与两个 LLM 主干上的实验表明，EvolveTrade 在多数评估设置中相比固定策略 LLM 基线提升了夏普比率与累计收益；行为分析显示自演化策略增加了代码介导的分析并激活了与市场状态相关的计算；案例级"策略—收益"归因追踪了策略诱导的配置变化如何贡献于已实现收益差异。

Conclusion: 自适应调整治理工具使用的可复用流程，是构建更稳健 LLM 交易代理的关键方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) trading agents can combine market data, news, and executable analysis, but their behavior is often controlled by static hand-written tool-use policies that are fixed before deployment. This limits their ability to adapt how they gather evidence, invoke tools, verify signals, and manage risk under changing market regimes. We introduce EvolveTrade, a self-evolving framework that treats the system prompt of a tool-using trading agent as a text-parameterized policy. After each update interval, a Policy Agent revises this policy using accumulated decision traces and realized portfolio feedback, while keeping the backbone LLM fixed. The updated policy is then used for the next batch of trading decisions, enabling the agent to refine its information-acquisition and portfolio-construction procedure over time. Experiments across multiple market regimes and two LLM backbones show that EvolveTrade often improves Sharpe Ratio and Cumulative Return over fixed-policy LLM baselines, achieving the improved SR and CR in most evaluated settings. Behavioral analyses further show that self-evolved policies increase code-mediated analysis and activate regime-relevant computations; case-level policy-to-return attributions trace how policy-induced allocation changes contribute to realized return differences. These results suggest that adapting the reusable procedure governing tool use is a key direction for building more robust LLM trading agents.

</details>


### [237] [One Color Preprocessing Improves DSATUR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17633)
*Adam Nouira,Lucas Isenmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SSLD，用半正定规划（SDP）预计算一个固定着色类，再交由DSATUR完成剩余顶点的着色，从而改进DSATUR的图着色质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图着色问题是NP-hard问题，DSATUR虽为最快启发式之一，但所用颜色数通常多于当前最优着色算法，因此存在改进其着色质量的空间。

Method: SSLD先通过一个类似计算Lovász theta数的半正定规划（SDP）求出一个优质颜色类作为预处理，将其固定后，再由DSATUR对剩余顶点完成着色，据称是首个通过预置固定颜色类来改进DSATUR的方法。

Result: 在DIMACS实例、随机图（Erdős–Rényi、Watts–Strogatz、Barabási–Albert）、频率分配及作业车间调度等超过1600个基准实例上，SSLD几乎在所有情况下追平或优于DSATUR，并优于朴素GISD基线，证实了SDP引导选择首个颜色类的价值。

Conclusion: SDP引导的首个颜色类预处理能有效改进DSATUR的着色质量，但运行时间约为DSATUR的195倍，表明该方向在质量和效率间的权衡上仍有进一步优化空间。

Abstract: The Graph Coloring Problem (GCP) is NP-hard and DSATUR stands as one of the fastest heuristics for it despite producing colorings that typically use more colors than state-of-the-art coloring algorithms. We propose SSLD (Semidefinite Spectral Learning with DSATUR), which improves DSATUR by preprocessing a first good color class before letting DSATUR complete coloring the rest of the given graph. We obtain this color class from a Semidefinite Programming (SDP), similar to an SDP used to compute the Lovász theta number. To the best of our knowledge, SSLD is the first approach to improve DSATUR by preprocessing through fixed color classes. We evaluate SSLD against DSATUR and against a naive 1-color-class preprocessing algorithm on DIMACS instances, random graphs (Erdős--Rényi, Watts-Strogatz, Barabási--Albert), Frequency Assignment and Job Shop Scheduling instances. SSLD matches or beats DSATUR in almost every case across over 1600 benchmark instances, and out performs the naive GISD baseline, allows us to confirm the value brought by the SDP-guided choice of the first color class. This quality comes at a runtime cost of roughly 195 times slower that DSATUR, but demonstrating that SDP-guided preprocessing of a first color class is a direction for future improvements.

</details>


### [238] [Physics-Constrained Digital Twins for Sensor Integrity in Urban Pedestrian Flow: Detecting Stealthy False Data Injection with Conformal Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17635)
*Oscar Mogollon Gutierrez,Fatemeh Ghasemi,Mohammadhossein Homaei,Andres Caro,Mar Avila*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将城市行人计数系统视为可被攻击的数据源，形式化了针对城市尺度行人传感的隐蔽虚假数据注入问题，构建了一个基于行人街道图的有向流数字孪生，并通过学习式图局部化增益与流守恒残差进行状态估计与攻击检测。在墨尔本六年数据上，定义"攻击裕度"衡量物理约束带来的鲁棒性增益：单设备被攻陷时裕度为0.54，三分之一设备被攻陷时降至0.19；将街道图换为距离图后裕度骤降至0.09，表明增益主要来自守恒律而非局部性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市行人计数系统已支撑经济指标、规划决策与安全运营，但基于其构建的数字孪生默认输入流为真值。作者研究输入被污染时的后果：行人传感中从潜流到观测的映射比电力、水网更严重的秩亏，已有的隐蔽性刻画不适用，因此需要针对该场景重新形式化隐蔽虚假数据注入问题。

Method: 1) 形式化城市尺度行人传感的隐蔽虚假数据注入；2) 数字孪生在行人街道图上估计有向流，通过学习的图局部化增益同化计数，并以耦合计量段与非计量段的流守恒残差进行训练；3) 检测结合新息与守恒残差，告警阈值由自适应保形校准设定而非人工设定；4) 定义攻击裕度——面向直接通过孪生优化的白盒对手，估计流场最坏情况污染的相对削减量。

Result: 在墨尔本六年数据上，仅1.18%可行走路段被计量的网络中，单设备被攻陷时攻击裕度达0.54；三分之一设备被攻陷时降至0.19。将街道图替换为距离图后裕度骤降至0.09，说明鲁棒性增益源于守恒律约束而非图局部性。

Conclusion: 流守恒残差是行人传感数字孪生获得攻击鲁棒性的核心来源，而非局部化增益结构；自适应保形校准提供了无需人工干预的检测阈值。攻击裕度可作为评估物理约束对隐蔽攻击防御价值的量化指标，该框架揭示了在低计量覆盖率下城市行人计数系统仍具备可利用的物理冗余。

Abstract: City pedestrian counting systems now feed economic indicators, planning decisions and safety operations, yet the twins built on top of them treat the incoming stream as ground truth. We study what happens when it is not. We formalise stealthy false data injection for city-scale pedestrian sensing, where the map from latent flow to observation is far more rank deficient than in the power and water networks for which stealth has been characterised. Our twin estimates directed flows on the pedestrian street graph, assimilates counts through a learned graph-localised gain, and is trained against a flow conservation residual that couples metered and unmetered segments. Detection combines the innovation with that residual, and the alarm threshold is set by adaptive conformal calibration rather than by hand. To measure what the physics buys, we define the attack margin, the relative reduction in worst-case corruption of the estimated flow field, achieved against a white-box adversary that optimises directly through the twin. On six years of Melbourne data the margin reaches 0.54 against a single compromised device and falls to 0.19 when a third of the fleet is compromised, on a network where only 1.18 per cent of walkable segments are metered. Replacing the street graph by a distance graph collapses it to 0.09, which shows that the gain comes from the conservation law rather than from locality.

</details>


### [239] [What You Can't See Is Still What You Learn: A Preregistered Sixty-Society Confirmation That Evidence Masking Drives Compositional Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17637)
*Narcis Marincat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过64个共享冻结语言模型主干、以学习到的连续数据包通信的四单元系统进行预注册验证，发现证据掩码机制在保留标记条件（及无标记复制）下大幅提升留出组合任务的准确率，但角色信息作用、中介归因与泛化性仍待解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 限制模块可读取的信息可能提升系统的计算学习能力。作者旨在检验这一假设：通过遮蔽外部证据，是否能改善多模块系统在组合任务上的学习效果，并厘清掩码效应的细粒度归因。

Method: 预注册确认性实验：64个四单元系统共享冻结的语言模型主干，通过学习到的连续数据包通信。五个条件操纵证据掩码、归属标记以及用中性填充替换外部证据；跨6个初始化聚类、每类2种数据顺序，在单一新任务世界中进行。另对18个审阅掩码系统进行数据包干预审计。

Result: 在两种含标记条件下，掩码将留出的二操作和三操作组合准确率中位配对差异分别提升0.846与0.859；全部12对达到要求边际，完整预注册行为标准通过，无标记复制也通过。无全局可见系统通过标记遵循检查，故角色信息作用未定。填充条件产生7个完全泛化系统，但其分解标准无定论。18个掩码系统中的数据包干预在合格案例上均符合预测的中间值变化，但该以成功为条件的有界审计不能确立中介作用。

Conclusion: 结果确认了所测掩码机制存在显著优势，但其更细粒度的归因与普遍性仍有待解答。协议、结果与检查点均已公开。

Abstract: Restricting what a module can read may improve what a system learns to compute. We test this in a preregistered confirmation with sixty four-cell systems sharing a frozen language-model backbone and communicating through learned continuous packets. Five conditions vary evidence masking, ownership markers, and replacement of foreign evidence with neutral filler, across six initialization clusters, each with two data orders, on one fresh task world. With markers available in both regimes, masking improved accuracy on held-out two- and three-operation compositions by median paired differences of 0.846 and 0.859; all twelve pairs cleared the required margins, and the full preregistered behavioral criterion passed. The unmarked replication also passed. No globally visible system passed the marker-following check, so the effect of usable role information remains unresolved. The filler condition yielded seven full generalizers, but its decomposition criteria were inconclusive. Packet interventions in all eighteen audited masked systems followed the predicted intermediate-value changes on eligible cases; these finite, success-conditioned audits do not establish mediation. The results confirm a large advantage of the tested masking regime, while leaving its finer attribution and generality open. Protocols, results, and checkpoints are public.

</details>


### [240] [CapMem: A Benchmark for Caption-Based Episodic Memory in Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17688)
*Dingli Liang,Yiqiao Xie,Yukai Huang,Zhaokai Wang,Weitong Cai,Guangwen Feng,Jifei Song,Zhensong Zhang,Hang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出以文本字幕作为可复用的情景记忆，定义CapMem基准任务，实验表明在长视频（>20分钟）上字幕问答优于直接视频问答，且检索-验证框架可进一步提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴助手需要对第一人称视频进行情景记忆，但现有视觉语言模型受限于帧预算、视觉token成本增长及长上下文检索失效，因此探索文本字幕能否作为可复用的情景记忆。

Method: 定义"情景记忆视频字幕问答（Episodic Memory Video Caption QA）"任务，构建CapMem人工标注基准（75个视频、33.7小时、1000道多选题、覆盖16个场景）；对比全覆盖CaptionQA（30s/60s字幕窗口）与直接VideoQA，并设置匹配帧控制实验及字幕引导的检索-验证框架。

Result: 在长视频（>20分钟）上，30s和60s窗口的CaptionQA分别在10/12和8/12模型上优于直接VideoQA；在相同视频子集上，六个Qwen模型的匹配帧控制平均提升3.22和2.55个百分点；检索-验证框架最多再提升5.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 字幕记忆对长第一人称视频的情景推理有效，可作为可复用的情景记忆形式。

Abstract: Wearable assistants require episodic memory over egocentric video, yet current vision-language models face bounded frame budgets, growing visual-token costs, and long-context retrieval failures. Under these practical constraints, we study whether textual captions can serve as reusable episodic memory. We define the Episodic Memory Video Caption QA task and introduce CapMem, a human-annotated benchmark with 75 videos totaling 33.7 hours, and 1,000 multiple-choice questions across 16 scenarios. On long videos (>20 min), full-coverage CaptionQA with 30s and 60s caption windows outperforms direct VideoQA for 10/12 and 8/12 models, respectively. On the same video subset, a matched-frame control across six Qwen models retains mean accuracy gains of 3.22 and 2.55 points, respectively. Our caption-guided retrieve-and-verify harness further improves accuracy by up to 5.3 points. These results support the effectiveness of caption memory for episodic reasoning over long egocentric video.

</details>


### [241] [GraphEcho: Structural Redundancy and Evidence Provenance in LLM Graph Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17695)
*Sikun Wang,Yixi Zhou,Lei Fan,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphEcho 基准测试揭示 LLM 智能体在图上探索时会错误地将重复遍历的路径当作独立证据，混淆"探索效率"与"证据有效性"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体能够在图上走过更多路径，但这些路径未必带来更多独立证据。重复遇到同一证据可能被智能体误认为是额外的佐证，从而影响其判断与探索行为。现有研究缺乏对"结论"与"探索是否触达独立证据源"两者的受控评估工具。

Method: 提出 GraphEcho 基准：在固定证据内容不变的前提下，改变路径数量与证据来源，评估智能体的判断与主动探索行为。同时在受控合成任务与科学论断任务上进行实验，并提出来源感知后训练（PAPT）方法以缓解重复问题。

Result: 受控合成实验显示，智能体判断变化依赖具体模型，但冗余支持路径在所有被评估的冻结智能体中都提高了重复遍历的占比。PAPT 能减少重访并提升合成任务准确率，但覆盖的来源更少；在科学论断任务上，它继续减少重复，但准确率反而下降。

Conclusion: 研究暴露了高效探索与有效证据利用之间的鸿沟：智能体可能学会停止重复自身，却忽视了真正需要的信息。GraphEcho 提供了一种受控方式，同时评估图智能体得出的结论及其探索是否触达独立证据源。

Abstract: A large language model (LLM) agent can follow more graph paths without acquiring more independent evidence. GraphEcho tests whether agents mistake these repeated encounters for additional corroboration. The benchmark varies path counts and evidential origins while holding evidence content fixed, and evaluates both judgments and active exploration. Controlled synthetic experiments reveal model-dependent judgment shifts, but redundant supporting paths increase the share of repeated walks across all evaluated frozen agents. Provenance-aware post-training (PAPT) reduces revisits and improves synthetic accuracy, yet covers fewer distinct sources. On scientific claims, it continues to reduce repetition while accuracy declines. These findings expose a gap between efficient exploration and effective evidence use: an agent can learn to stop repeating itself while overlooking information it needs. GraphEcho provides a controlled way to evaluate both what graph agents conclude and whether their exploration reaches distinct evidential sources.

</details>


### [242] [GVD: Governed Versioning and Deduplication for Document Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17696)
*Mohammadreza Sediqin,Shivali Dalmia,Sumukha Thoppanahalli,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GVD框架，将跨文档版本链接与规则级冲突消解统一在可审计的更新策略下，实现重复、矛盾、非对称细化与新知识的自动识别，且无需大型语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档库持续演化，指南与政策被修订、替换和重新上传，同一内容以不同措辞重现，新版本细化或推翻旧版本。这些不一致性属于整个文档集合而非单篇文档，但现有工作把版本管理、重复检测和矛盾检测当作孤立的成对任务，一旦完成配对标注即终止，缺乏全局统一处理。

Method: GVD通过双向规则对齐将新入文档分配到版本族；将其规则与族记忆比较，识别重复、矛盾、非对称细化和新知识；用反事实片段探测（CSP）解决推理误判为中性的相关配对；采用关系特定策略抑制重复、仅升级重要变更以供审核，并保留版本谱系作为审计追踪。整个流水线本地运行，不使用大型语言模型。

Result: 在120份企业文档、59个版本族、140次摄入上，版本族构建F1达0.97，规则级一致性F1达0.94；CSP将规则一致性从0.90提升至0.94。

Conclusion: GVD将版本链接与规则级冲突消解统一为可审计的更新策略，在无大模型条件下取得高精度，验证了跨文档版本治理与去重的可行性。

Abstract: Document repositories evolve continuously. Guidelines and policies are revised, superseded, and re-uploaded, so the same content recurs in different wording and newer versions refine or contradict earlier ones. These inconsistencies belong to the growing collection rather than to any single document, yet existing work treats versioning, duplicate detection, and contradiction detection as isolated pairwise tasks and stops once a pair is labeled. We present GVD (Governed Versioning and Deduplication), a framework that unifies cross-document version linking with rule-level conflict resolution under an auditable update policy. Incoming documents are assigned to version families through bidirectional rule alignment, and their rules are compared against the family memory to identify duplicates, contradictions, asymmetric refinements, and new knowledge, with Counterfactual Span Probing (CSP) resolving related pairs that inference misclassifies as neutral. Relation-specific policies suppress duplicates and escalate only consequential changes for review, retaining version lineage as an audit trail. The pipeline runs fully locally, with no large language model.
